<a id="pn-3-2012"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 3/2012 — High-tier data centre lease modification / data centre waiver
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
18 June 2012 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Issue No. 3/2012 | Window: **25 June 2012 – 31 March 2016** (both dates inclusive)

> Scope note: Two time-limited tracks for **industrial lots / industrial buildings**: (a) lease modification or land exchange to develop the lot (wholly or in part) as **high-tier data centre**; (b) **nil-fee waiver** to use **part of an existing industrial building** as **data centre**. It is **not** a BO plan code, FSD circular, or a standing open window after 31 March 2016. Special industrial lots (industrial estates, container-terminal storage, HA flatted factories) are excluded.

---

## Regulatory Overview

An **industrial lot** is one that, under the Government lease, may not be used for any purpose other than **industrial or godown purposes or both**. Applications under this PN may be submitted only in the window in paragraph 1; postal chop (or LandsD receipt) is the submission date. Lease modification and Data Centre Waiver **may not be submitted concurrently**.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. High-tier data centre lease modification — intensity and premium (paras 2–5, fn 4)

| Lock | Rule |
|---|---|
| Intensity | Up to **or less than** max permissible under the statutory town plan, or **BO** if the plan is silent |
| Data-centre quantum | Data-centre part **not less than 40%** of applicable max intensity **or PR 2.5**, **whichever is the higher** |
| Ancillary office | **Not more than 30%** of total usable floor area of the data-centre part |
| Premium | Difference between land value under current lease vs proposed modified lease; data-centre part assessed as **“high-tier data centre”** use |
| Fees | Admin fee in **two instalments** (on demand at submission; balance on acceptance of binding basic terms) + LACO fee if applicable; non-refundable as set out in demand letters |

**High-tier data centre (fn 4):** designed for concurrent maintenance (uninterrupted planned maintenance) and high availability; typically redundant telecom access, redundant support components (generator, cooling), redundant power and cooling distribution paths, dedicated environmentally controlled computer-room space, dedicated cooling, dedicated generator and fuel tank. Redundant systems take over primary; dedicated plant **not shared** with non-data-centre uses.

**SD takeaway:** Size the data-centre GFA at **≥ 40% of max intensity or PR 2.5 (higher of the two)** and keep ancillary office **≤ 30% of DC usable floor area** — LandsD will write max intensity (including DC part) into the modification letter.

---

### 2. Data Centre Waiver — part of an existing IB (paras 6–12, fn 1, 5)

| Lock | Rule |
|---|---|
| Zoning | IB in **“Industrial”**, **“Commercial”** or **“OU(B)”** on statutory town plans |
| Age | IB **not less than 15 years** at submission (from date of **last-issued OP**) |
| Fee | **Nil waiver fee** |
| Term | Lifetime of the IB **or** expiry/termination of current Government lease, **whichever earlier** |
| User during waiver | **No part** of the Industrial Premises may be used for any use **other than data centre** |
| Building works | Must comply with **BO**; complete within **3 years** of waiver letter; AP-certified proof (e.g. BA acknowledgement of **Form BA 14**) |
| DMC | Waiver does **not** prejudice DMC rights/remedies |
| Other lease terms | Waiver only lifts **user restriction**; other lease changes need a **separate** lease modification (premium as LandsD considers appropriate) |
| Revert / vary | Cancel waiver to revert to original lease user; change of waiver conditions or area needs a fresh LandsD application (premium/charge at discretion) |
| Later modification | Full market premium **without** benefit of the waiver |

**Data centre (fn 1):** facility housing computer systems, servers, telecom equipment and associated support (UPS, power distribution, standby generation, heat rejection, CRAC, fire suppression, environmental and security control) in a secure, controlled environment.

**Industrial building (fn 5):** lawfully erected and standing at submission date on a lot restricted to industrial or godown or both; same special-factory exclusions as industrial lots.

**SD takeaway:** For conversion of part of an existing IB, lock **≥15-year** building age, **I / C / OU(B)** zoning, and a **3-year** works-completion programme — the waiver dies with the building and does not credit later premium.

---

### 3. Process, existing applications, and existing waivers (paras 13–18)

| Item | Rule |
|---|---|
| Submit to | LandsD HQ, 19/F North Point Government Offices, 333 Java Road — **RCIB** |
| Professionals | Engage competent professionals |
| Original vs New Application | If a lease-mod application was already in hand **before 25 June 2012**, owner may (within the window) file a New Application under (a) or (b) and ask LandsD to **defer** the Original; if New is executed as (a), Original is treated as withdrawn; if New is (b), Original continues only on **written request** |
| Fees on switch | Original admin + LACO fees **not refunded**; New Application attracts its own fees |
| Existing waiver | May be replaced by Data Centre Waiver; existing waiver **terminated on execution**; **no** refund of waiver fees or admin fees; **no** termination notice |
| Concurrent tracks | Lease modification **and** Data Centre Waiver **may not** be submitted together |
| Other licences | FSD, BA and other authorities remain required |

**SD takeaway:** Pick **one** track (redevelopment modification vs in-situ waiver); do not run both, and do not assume an existing unit waiver survives or is refunded.

---

### Source

- `2012_3.pdf` (LAO PN Issue No. 3/2012, 18 June 2012)
- Read with: **LAO PN 3/2012A** and **3/2012B** (later variations — 3/2012B supersedes Appendix I); Town Planning Board / OZP user; BO and FSD for works and licences
*Source Critical Summary:* `source_md/LAO Practice Note No. 3_2012 (June 2012)_CS.md`

<a id="pn-3-2012a"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 3/2012A — Data Centre Applications after 31 March 2016
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
3 March 2016 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Varies and supplements LAO PN 3/2012 **from 1 April 2016**

> Scope note: **Extends the application window** for (a) lease modification / land exchange of an industrial lot for **high-tier data centre**, or (b) **waiver** for data-centre use in **part of** an industrial building, **after 31 March 2016 until further notice**. All other 3/2012 provisions remain. It is **not** the technical definition of “high-tier data centre” — that stays in PN 3/2012 / 3/2012B.

## Regulatory Overview

Without this PN, the 3/2012 scheme would have stopped taking applications after 31 March 2016. Caption (b) waiver processing is to be **streamlined toward about two weeks** from a valid application; details to follow by later amendment (delivered in **3/2012B**).

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. Window and status of 3/2012 (paras. 1–2)

| Item | Rule |
|---|---|
| Effect | From **1 April 2016** |
| Applications | May be made **after 31 March 2016 until further Government notice** |
| Parent | Except as varied here, **PN 3/2012 remains valid**; together = “the Practice Notes” |
| Waiver (caption b) | Aim: approval of acceptable cases **around two weeks** from valid application (details later) |

**SD takeaway:** Post-March 2016 IB data-centre conversion still uses the **3/2012 family** (now 3/2012 + 3/2012A + 3/2012B) — do not treat 31 March 2016 as a hard stop.

### Source

`2012A_3.pdf` | LAO PN 3/2012, 3/2012B. Text from OCR of a scanned PDF.

<a id="pn-3-2012b"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 3/2012B — High-tier data centre / data centre waiver (streamlining)
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
May 2016 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Issue No. 3/2012B | Immediate effect | Varies and supplements **LAO PN 3/2012** and **3/2012A**

> Scope note: Process and documentation update for the same two tracks as PN 3/2012. Caption (b) **includes part of, parts of, or an entire industrial building**. It does **not** restate the 3/2012 intensity or 15-year/zoning locks — those remain in 3/2012 / 3/2012A unless varied here. Appendix I here **supersedes** Appendix I to PN 3/2012.

---

## Regulatory Overview

Except as varied by this PN, all other provisions of PN 3/2012 and 3/2012A remain valid. The three notes are collectively “the Practice Notes”. The stated aim is to issue caption-(b) waivers acceptable to Government, on terms along the sample letter at Appendix II, **around two weeks** from receipt of a **valid** application with all required documents.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. What this PN changes (paras 1–2, fn 1)

| Item | Change |
|---|---|
| Caption (b) coverage | Part of, **parts of, or an entire** industrial building |
| Form | Application **must** be in the form of **Appendix I** plus Checklist of Basic Requirements |
| Routing | **(a)** lease modification / exchange → **District Lands Officer**; **(b)** waiver → **Chief Estate Surveyor/Headquarters**, 20/F North Point Government Offices |
| Waiver target time | **~2 weeks** from valid receipt (documents complete) |
| Sample instrument | Appendix II sample **Data Centre Waiver** letter |

**SD takeaway:** Treat 3/2012B as the current **application pack and waiver form** — submit (b) to CES/HQ with the new Appendix I, not the 3/2012 form.

---

### 2. Application pack — design-facing extras vs 3/2012 (Appendix I checklist)

| Must-submit (beyond 3/2012 pack) | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Energy saving plan** | For the planned data centre; refer to OGCIO **“Green Data Centre Practices”** (fn 4) |
| Waiver floor plans | **4 copies**, not larger than **A3**, property edged **pink**, **internal floor area** specified; AP or other competent professional |
| Caption (b) declarations | Zoning **I / C / OU(B)** with OZP No.; latest **OP No.** and building age **≥ 15 years** from that OP |
| Caption (b) termination clause | Applicant accepts waiver may be terminated and **admin fee not refunded** even after grant |
| Lease-mod intensity | Still declare if proposed intensity is **less than** max, with GFA and **% of max** |
| High-tier proof | Conceptual design satisfying **fn 4 of PN 3/2012** |

**SD takeaway:** Freeze **pink-edged IFA plans**, an **energy-saving / Green Data Centre** narrative, and OP-dated **≥15-year** age before filing — missing DVD-style completeness is not the issue here; missing pink plans and green plan are.

---

### 3. Sample waiver — design and works locks (Appendix II)

| Condition | Lock |
|---|---|
| Permitted purpose | **Data centre** as defined (computers, servers, telecom + E&M support in a secure controlled environment) |
| Warranties | Zoning I / C / OU(B); building age **≥ 15 years** from **latest OP** at application date — false/misleading → Government may terminate |
| Term | From letter date until **demolition of existing building**, lease expiry date, or **early lease termination** — whichever earlier |
| Exclusive user | Premises **only** for Permitted Purpose; Director’s determination **final** |
| Works | BO-compliant; complete within **3 years**; AP-certified proof **≤ 2 calendar months** after that 3-year period; fail → Government may terminate |
| Traffic | Before works start, supply TD-required information that road access, parking, and L/UL are adequate and no adverse traffic impact |
| Licences | All Government licences/permits **before** operation |
| Premium later | Subsequent redevelopment or lease modification assessed **without** taking the waiver into account |
| Resumption | Government may terminate on **≥ 1 calendar month** notice after resumption notice |
| DMC / mortgage | Waiver does not prejudice co-owners’ DMC rights or chargee/mortgagee rights; chargee consent to be endorsed if charged |
| Existing waiver | This waiver **supersedes** any existing waiver on the Premises from letter date |
| Not a lease mod | All other lease covenants remain; waiver is **not** a modification |

**SD takeaway:** Programme conversion works to **finish in 3 years** with AP proof, and size parking/L/UL for TD before start on site — the waiver is personal to this building’s life and is ignored in any later premium.

---

- `2012B_3.pdf` (LAO PN Issue No. 3/2012B, May 2016)
- Read with: **LAO PN 3/2012** (intensity, 15-year, 40%/PR 2.5, 30% office); **LAO PN 3/2012A**; OGCIO Green Data Centre Practices

<a id="pn-4-2014"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 4/2014 — Accountable and non-accountable GFA under lease
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
August 2014 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Issue No. 4/2014 | Supersedes APSRSE **1/98(A)**, LAO **5/2000**, **7/2002**, and APSS **3/99**

> Scope note: LandsD practice (as landlord) on which building elements are **GFA-accountable or exempt under lease**. BA statutory GFA and LandsD lease GFA are **separate**; LandsD **usually follows BA** where the lease gives the Director discretion, but the Director may do otherwise in individual cases. It is **not** a BO GFA code. Later **PN 4/2014A** + **JPN No. 4** (from 18 October 2021) overlay accountability for certain facilities on new/major-revision plans.

---

## Regulatory Overview

Any building on the lot is measured for GFA unless the lease exempts it. Paras 4–38 are **guides only** to elements that may be exempted. Leases without an express exemption usually need **lease modification** before the same BA exemption can be taken under lease.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. Default measurement and “follow BA” (paras 1–3, 39)

| Rule | Detail |
|---|---|
| Default | All buildings measured for GFA unless lease-exempted |
| Discretionary exemptions | If lease reserves Director’s right to exempt, LandsD **usually** follows BA, Director retaining absolute discretion otherwise |
| Appeal | Director may consider GFA-exemption appeals; reasons required; Director’s decision **final** |

**SD takeaway:** Do not assume BA GFA exemption = lease GFA exemption — check the lease clause first; if silent, budget a **modification**.

---

### 2. Plant, vertical circulation, headroom, roofs (paras 4–10)

| Element | Lease GFA lock |
|---|---|
| E&M plant | Areas occupied **solely** by lift / A/C / heating (or similar) machinery may be exempt; BA usually followed |
| Stairs / lifts / lobbies serving **only** non-accountable GFA (e.g. basement/underground parking, mechanical floors) | May be exempt if **physically separated**, no access to GFA-accountable floors, design/disposition acceptable to Director |
| Same features serving basement **and** other floors | Only the **basement/underground parking portion** may be exempt; **entrance lift lobby and/or lift shaft at and above ground** serving the development **and** basement **included** in GFA |
| Composite: stairs/lifts/lobbies solely serving upper **domestic** | **Domestic GFA** even if they pass through non-domestic floors; if they also serve non-domestic GFA, count as **either** domestic or non-domestic GFA |
| Floor-to-floor | **3.5 m** flats / **4.5 m** houses generally acceptable; excess headroom **rejected or double (or more) counted** unless justified |
| High ceilings that may be accepted | Voids over living/dining/entrance foyer in duplex/house; mall atriums; banking halls; sporting halls; hotel main entrance lobbies — design must not invite abuse or lease breach; **BA approval** required; BA usually followed |
| Balconies / similar (cantilevered or on stilts) | **GFA accountable** |
| Flat roofs as natural set-back on roof of GFA-accountable areas | May be **non-accountable**; BA usually followed |
| Flat roof on GFA-**exempt** structure | Non-accountable if **DMC common**; private flat roof/garden may be non-accountable if area **≈ 1:1** with the flat/house it serves; if **> 1:1**, excess is GFA unless reduced to ~1:1 **or** excess designated **DMC common** |
| Open-sided covered landscape (residential, **not** single residence; or residential portion of composite) | BA usually followed if design, disposition and height acceptable to Director |

**SD takeaway:** Keep basement lift/stair cores **separated** from tower GFA, hold flat/house F-to-F at **3.5 / 4.5 m**, and cap private roof gardens at **~1:1** or put the excess in the DMC common parts.

---

### 3. Ancillary rooms, extra parking, walkways, yards (paras 11–20)

| Element | Lease GFA lock |
|---|---|
| Watchmen / caretakers’ office | Many existing residential / residential-portion leases permit and exempt; **new sale/tender** sites may use a **different** exemption quantum — read the lease. No clause → **modification**. BA (location/design) usually followed |
| OC / owners’ committee office | Same pattern; **not** applicable to **single residence**. No clause → **modification** |
| Extra parking / L/UL beyond lease min (incl. flexibility) + associated circulation/stairs; undesignated areas on car-park floors | **GFA accountable**; Director may exempt extras/undesignated space from **design constraints** if designated **DMC common** |
| Open-sided covered walkways linking residential blocks at GF or podium | May be excluded if location, alignment and width to Director’s satisfaction |
| Covered walkways **required under lease** within podium | Exempt **if** the lease so provides and they fully comply |
| Covered walkway **fronting shopping units** | **GFA accountable** even if open-sided, unless lease says otherwise |
| Uncovered light wells / yards / similar (not easy to abuse) | May be exempt; Director may follow BA. **3- or 4-side enclosed** open areas likely to be abused → generally **GFA** even if BA exempts |
| 3-side enclosed open flat roof / garden / roof / terrace | GFA if abuse-likely, unless **DMC common**. Private use more acceptable if facing common area (visible to residents/management) or between two flats with common parapet **≤ 1.5 m** |

**SD takeaway:** Shop-front covered walks and extra car-park voids are **in GFA** unless the lease or a DMC-common/design-constraint concession says otherwise — do not copy BA exemptions blindly.

---

### 4. Swimming pools and stair access (paras 21–30)

| Element | Lease GFA lock |
|---|---|
| Open pool on **garden-use** lots | Permissible **without** modification if **at grade** and ancillary structures do not exceed max GFA/SC or other lease conditions |
| Flatted / composite residential / estate houses — **communal** open pool | May be GFA-exempt if **DMC common**, reasonable size/design, all residents |
| **Private** open pools | Reasonable size/design; generally **one pool per block** (flatted/composite residential) or **one pool per house**, **in addition to** communal pool(s); “one per block/house” may be relaxed if **at grade**. Merits: type, size, design, disposition, height, environmental impact |
| No standard Recreational Facilities clause | Paras 22–23 principles still apply |
| Non-residential **other than hotels** | Open private/communal pool (at grade or not) + ancillaries **GFA accountable** unless lease specifically exempts |
| Maintenance void under pool/deck | **≤ 700 mm** clear headroom exempt if BA accepts; **> 700 mm** may be accepted with AP justification + BA. Maintenance corridor **≤ 1.1 m** clear (private) or **≤ 1.5 m** clear (communal) under/adjacent to deck generally exempt if BA accepts |
| Open external staircase as **main access** to unit/house (to save internal GFA) | **GFA accountable** even if BA exempts |
| Open **private** stair from top-floor unit to private roof (plus communal stair/stairhood in same block) | Normally **non-accountable**; BA usually followed |
| **Private stairhood** at roof (plus communal stair/stairhood) | **GFA accountable** |
| Communal stairhood (maintenance / fire escape) | Exempt if size and number reasonable; BA usually followed |

**SD takeaway:** Count private roof **stairhoods** and open **main-access** stairs in GFA; communal open pools need a **DMC common** designation and stay within one-private-pool-per-block/house unless at grade.

---

### 5. Envelope, green features, hotel BOH, clubhouse (paras 31–38)

| Element | Lease GFA lock |
|---|---|
| Curtain wall (modern lease with exemption in “Definition of GFA and SC”) | Usually follow BA / **PNAP APP-2** (projection limits from outer face of structure) |
| Curtain wall **without** lease exemption | Literal GFA from **actual external faces** — BA APP-2 measurement will **exceed** lease GFA → **modification** required |
| JPN 1 & 2 features (balcony, utility platform, non-structural prefab wall, noise barrier, communal sky/podium garden, etc.) | Modern leases usually exempt; **pre-requisite** = BA exemption first; BA usually followed. No lease clause → **modification** (after BA exemption) |
| Bay windows / A/C platforms | Exempt if lease so provides; BA usually followed |
| Canopies | Permitted if design acceptable, unlikely to be abused, and lease exemption exists; BA area usually followed |
| Hotel **back-of-house** | Unless lease **requires** BOH in overall/hotel GFA, BA exemption usually followed |
| Recreational facilities / clubhouse | Indoor, appropriate scale/composition, common use by residents and bona fide visitors under standard Recreational Facilities clause may be exempt; types per **LAO PN 4/2000(B)**; BA active/passive/ancillary mix usually followed |

**SD takeaway:** On older leases, **curtain wall and JPN features need modification** even if BA already exempted them — lock the GFA definition clause at SD before committing to a unitised curtain wall.

---

### Source

- `2014_4.pdf` (LAO PN Issue No. 4/2014, August 2014; paras 40–41 recovered by OCR)
- Read with: **LAO PN 4/2014A** and **JPN No. 4** (streamlined GFA accountability from 18 October 2021); **PNAP APP-2**; **JPN Nos. 1 & 2**; **LAO PN 4/2000(B)**
*Source Critical Summary:* `source_md/LAO Practice Note No. 4_2014 (August 2014)_CS.md`

---

<a id="pn-4-2014a"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 4/2014A — Accountable and non-accountable GFA under lease (JPN 4 overlay)
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
7 October 2021 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Issue No. 4/2014A | Varies/supplements **LAO PN 4/2014**

> Scope note: Implements **revised Joint Practice Note No. 4** streamlined GFA/PR accountability among BD, LandsD and PlanD. It **does not** rewrite PN 4/2014 for every element; it substitutes JPN 4 (and BA’s ruling) for **certain** facilities/features/structures on plans first submitted (or major-revised) on or after the Effective Date. **Not** applicable to buildings exempted from Cap. 123 by **Cap. 121**.

---

## Regulatory Overview

JPN No. 4 takes effect from **18 October 2021** (the Effective Date). Streamlined GFA accountability applies to **all new building plans or major revision of building plans** submitted to BA for approval **on or after** that date. For those plans, GFA accountability **under lease** for the covered facilities follows JPN 4 (and its exemption criteria, if any), **notwithstanding** PN 4/2014; in principle BA’s ruling is followed.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. When JPN 4 displaces PN 4/2014 (paras 1–3)

| Item | Rule |
|---|---|
| Effective Date | **18 October 2021** |
| Plans covered | New GBP **or major revision** submitted to BA **on or after** Effective Date |
| Accountability | For certain facilities, features and structures: treat GFA **under lease** per **JPN No. 4** (subject to JPN 4 exemption criteria) |
| BA | In principle, **BA’s ruling followed** for exemption from GFA |
| Cap. 121 | This PN **does not apply** to buildings exempted from Cap. 123 by Cap. 121 |
| Lease rights | Nothing fetters Government/Director as landlord; not a waiver of lease |

**SD takeaway:** For schemes whose first (or major-revision) BA submission is **on/after 18 October 2021**, size typical facilities to **JPN 4 + BA**, not the older PN 4/2014 tables — unless the building is Cap. 121-exempt.

---

### Source

- `PN 4_2014A.pdf` (LAO PN Issue No. 4/2014A, 7 October 2021)
- Read with: **LAO PN 4/2014** (still the parent for cases outside JPN 4’s plan trigger); **revised JPN No. 4**; **Cap. 121** if NT-exempt building
*Source Critical Summary:* `source_md/LAO Practice Note No. 4_2014A (October 2021)_CS.md`

<a id="pn-1-2016"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 1/2016 — Waiver for testing laboratory use in industrial buildings
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
25 January 2016 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Issue No. 1/2016 | Applies from **1 February 2016** until LandsD otherwise determines

> Scope note: **Nil-fee waiver** to use part(s) of, or an **entire existing** industrial building, as **testing and/or calibration laboratory** (in addition to lease-permitted user) for the building’s lifetime or lease expiry/termination, whichever earlier. **Clinical** labs that require humans or animals to undergo checking **on the premises** are **not** eligible. Vacant sites cannot apply. Not a BO/FSD code.

---

## Regulatory Overview

An industrial building here is one lawfully erected on a lot restricted to **industrial and/or godown** purposes (excluding special factories: container-terminal storage, HA flatted factories, and special-industry lots such as cargo handling, ship building/repair, oil storage/refining and associated chemicals, polystyrene, chlorine/hydrogen/textile chemicals, etc.). Planning: testing-lab use must be always-permitted on the current statutory plan, **or** have **TPB permission before** LandsD filing (proof at application), **or** qualify as existing use under the statutory plans. Director’s decision on whether a use is “testing laboratory” is **final**.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. Waiver locks (paras 2–7)

| Item | Rule |
|---|---|
| Fee | **Nil waiver fee** (admin fee still payable on demand; non-refundable if withdrawn or not executed) |
| Term | Lifetime of **existing** IB at issue date **or** lease expiry/termination, whichever earlier |
| Clinical exclusion | Labs needing humans/animals to undergo checking **at the Industrial Premises** — **not eligible** for nil-fee waiver |
| Works | Any works for the permitted lab use must comply with **BO** |
| DMC | Waiver does not prejudice DMC rights/remedies |
| Parties | Executed by owner **and** mortgagees/chargees **and** ASP purchasers |
| What is waived | **User restriction** only vs testing-lab use; other lease changes need **separate** modification |
| Later mod / other waiver | Premium or waiver fee at **full market value without** benefit of this waiver |
| Vary area/conditions | Fresh LandsD application; premium/charge at discretion |
| Other authorities | FSD, BA and others still required |

**SD takeaway:** Do not programme **on-premises clinical** testing (human/animal subjects) onto this nil-fee track; get **TPB** first if the OZP requires permission, then treat the waiver as **user-only** for the life of this building.

---

### 2. Existing waivers and filing (paras 8–11)

| Item | Rule |
|---|---|
| Submit to | **Relevant DLO** (LandsD website contacts) |
| Existing waiver | May be replaced; terminated **on execution** of Testing Laboratory Waiver; **no** refund of waiver or admin fees; **no** termination notice |
| Discretion | Each case on merits as landlord |

**SD takeaway:** An existing unit waiver will be **killed without refund** when this lab waiver is executed — do not rely on stacking waivers.

---

### Source

- `PN 1_2016.pdf` (LAO PN Issue No. 1/2016, 25 January 2016)
- Read with: OZP / TPB permission; BO and FSD; **LAO PN 4/2019** (other industrial-premises waivers expressly point here for testing labs)
*Source Critical Summary:* `source_md/LAO Practice Note No. 1_2016 (January 2016)_CS.md`

<a id="pn-2-2016"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 2/2016 — Consent to disposal under entire-IB conversion special waiver
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
1 June 2016 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Issue No. 2/2016

> Scope note: Streamlines **Director’s prior written consent to sell/assign/dispose** required throughout the validity of a **special waiver for conversion of an entire existing industrial building** issued under **LAO PN 1/2010, 1/2010A and 1/2010B**. It is **not** a design/GFA PN and does not itself grant conversion rights.

---

## Regulatory Overview

The special waiver requires the Assignor, before disposal, to obtain the Director’s prior written consent and to procure a sealed **undertaking** from the Assignee (in favour of Government and the Director) to observe the waiver, and if the lot is in multiple ownership to be jointly and/or severally liable with other owners. This PN deems that consent given if the specified application and undertaking path is followed, and drops Assignee chargee/mortgagee endorsement of the undertaking.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. Deemed consent and undertaking (paras 1–4, Appendices I–II)

| Item | Rule |
|---|---|
| Deemed consent | Director’s consent to sell/assign/dispose is **deemed given** if, **before** sale, conveyance or any agreement so to do, Assignor files Appendix I application **and** complies with special-waiver conditions on procuring and delivering the Appendix II undertaking |
| Appendix I trigger | On LandsD receipt of the form, consent required under **Condition 10(a)(i)** of the waiver letter is deemed given **provided** Assignor complies with **Conditions 10(a)(ii) and 10(b)** |
| Assignee chargee | Undertaking **no longer** needs endorsement by Assignee’s chargee/mortgagee |
| In-hand letters | Waiver letters **not yet issued** will reflect paras 2–3 |
| Already granted waivers | LandsD issues a **Relaxation Letter** (no fees/charges) before or upon any consent-to-disposal application |
| Chain of title | Assignee may only further dispose on (i) Director’s consent and (ii) procuring a **similar** undertaking from the next purchaser; undertaking registered **contemporaneously** with the assignment |
| Incomplete conversion works | Undertaking may include completing all waiver-permitted works by the stipulated date (and any extension letter date) |

**SD takeaway:** Conversion special-waiver units remain **covenant-bound on every assignment** — programme the AP/legal pack so the Appendix II undertaking (and any outstanding conversion-works date) is ready **before** ASP, not after.

---

### Source

- `PN 2_2016.pdf` (LAO PN Issue No. 2/2016, 1 June 2016)
- Read with: **LAO PN 1/2010, 1/2010A, 1/2010B** (parent special waiver); **LAO PN 6/2019** (later entire-IB special waiver also applies this PN’s disposal procedures)
*Source Critical Summary:* `source_md/LAO Practice Note No. 2_2016 (June 2016)_CS.md`

<a id="pn-1-2017"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 1/2017 — Building Plans on Virtually Unrestricted Leases
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
12 April 2017 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department

> Scope note: **Simplified LandsD “no objection”** for GBP on a **virtually unrestricted** Government lease (no development/user restrictions other than **offensive trades**). It is **not** a BO concession PN and **does not** apply if ROW, rate-and-range, DDH/DD, or LandsD plan-approval clauses exist — those stay on the **8-week** PN 5/2002 path.

## Regulatory Overview

BD still refers plans via PNAP ADM-2. LandsD will issue a **no-objection letter** if: (a) the lease is virtually unrestricted (Director’s decision **final**); (b) **no encroachment** onto Government land; (c) proposed uses do not breach the **offensive-trade** restriction (or any offensive-trade **licence**). AP lodges Appendix II form + full lease pack at **first submission**.

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. What “virtually unrestricted” means (para. 2 + fn. 1, para. 6)

| In | Out (usual processing; simplified approach **off**) |
|---|---|
| Lease with **no** specific development/user restrictions other than offensive trades (sample: App. I) | **Rights of way** clause |
| Offensive-trade clause may exist — see LAO PN **6/2007** | **Rate and range** clause |
| | **DDH or DD** clause |
| | Requirement for **Director’s approval of building plans** / erection other than existing buildings |
| | Site with **>1 lot or section** where **any** lease is **not** virtually unrestricted |

**SD takeaway:** Confirm the lease is **truly bare** (plus offensive trades only) before assuming a LandsD no-objection shortcut — one DDH or ROW clause kills it.

### 2. AP pack (paras. 3–5, Apps. II–III)

| Need | Detail |
|---|---|
| Form App. II Parts I–III | Virtually unrestricted declaration; offensive-trade licence compliance; **full certified lease set** (modifications, licences) + ownership printouts — certified by LR or solicitor **within 3 months** of submission |
| Parts IV–V | Confirm **no encroachment** on Government land (exception: architectural/amenity projections needing BD **and** LandsD approval) |
| Development schedule | Uses **per floor**; sample App. III (adapted from APSRSE **1/94**). Shaded fields (height SC, etc.) **not required** / may be deleted if lease is virtually unrestricted |
| ALS | If using land survey as site-parameter proof (PNAP ADV-33): ALS name, plan, report, record plan, SMF-0043 already at BD |

**SD takeaway:** Put a **floor-by-floor user schedule** on the drawings at first GBP — LandsD’s no-objection rests on it plus the certified lease bundle.

### 3. Street widening / voluntary surrender (para. 8)

If part of the lot is needed for **street widening**, AP may propose **voluntary surrender** in exchange for B(P)R **22(2)** concessions (PNAP APP-20). Show it on plans **and** Development Schedule; approach LandsD **Acquisition Section**. No-objection still needs para. 2 **plus** Acquisition Section / department acceptance of the surrender.

**SD takeaway:** Road-widening concessions on these lots are a **parallel Acquisition** track — do not assume no-objection includes the surrender.

### 4. Timing if simplified approach does not apply (para. 7)

Non-BCIII GBP under lease remains **8 weeks** per LAO PN **5/2002**.

**SD takeaway:** Mixed lots (one restricted lease in the assemblage) → full 8-week LandsD check.

### Source

`PN 1_2017.pdf` | PNAP ADM-2, ADV-33, APP-20 | LAO PN 5/2002, 6/2007, APSRSE 1/94. Body from OCR; App. I historic lease is image-garbled — use the PDF.
*Source Critical Summary:* `source_md/LAO Practice Note No. 1-2017 (12 April 2017)_CS.md`

<a id="pn-2-2018"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 2/2018 — Processing of general building plans under lease
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
26 November 2018 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Issue No. 2/2018 | Supersedes **LAO PN 1/1994**

> Scope note: Landlord-side **GBP** processing through BD’s Centralized Processing System. Sets **rejection-without-scrutiny** gates (MLP, Development Schedule, colouring of amendments, separate lease GFA/SC calcs). It is **not** a BO submission PN. Sample schedule is **not** for virtually unrestricted redevelopment under **LAO PN 1/2017**.

---

## Regulatory Overview

LandsD comments and decides on GBP referred through the Centralized Processing System. A submission that fails paragraph 1(i)–(iv) is **rejected under lease without scrutiny**. Incomplete or incorrect Development Schedule may likewise be rejected without scrutiny. Preliminary comments should go via enquiry or workshop under **LAO PN 4/2018**.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. Mandatory submission gates (para 1)

| Gate | Rule |
|---|---|
| (i) MLP | **Prior approval** of Master Layout Plan **if required** |
| (ii) Development Schedule | Accompany GBP; sample Appendix I; Guidance Notes Appendix II |
| (iii) Re-submission / amendment | Amended parts **suitably coloured** |
| (iv) GFA / SC | **Separate** lease GFA and SC calculations unless they are **the same** as under the BO |

**SD takeaway:** Do not send GBP to LandsD until MLP (if any) is approved and the Development Schedule + lease GFA/SC (if different from BO) are complete — missing any gate is a **without-scrutiny reject**.

---

### 2. Development Schedule — what the AP must lock (Appendix I & II)

| Schedule block | Design lock |
|---|---|
| Location / lot / site area | Approx. m² |
| Height | Storeys, proposed height vs lease restriction, SC ref, drawing no. |
| Part D items | User; type of building; **GFA**; **SC**; building **separation**; building **setback**; **greenery**; DDH/DD (see **LAO PN 3/2014**); carpark; L/UL; vehicular access; caretaker office/quarters; OC/owners’ committee office; recreational facilities; NBA (e.g. drainage/waterworks reserve); formation areas (Green, Yellow, etc.); tree preservation; landscaping; other SCs (footbridge, open space, etc.) |
| MLP | Date of approved MLP if applicable |
| Lease documents | Use **latest** complete lease from Land Registry |
| Block plan | Mark lot boundary, vehicular access / footbridge points, NBAs, setback, coloured areas (Green / Yellow / Brown) **inside and outside** the lot per lease plan; site coordinates next to Block Plan (typically **1:1000**) |
| “Required/permitted” column | State stipulated parameters (e.g. **max and min GFA** under total GFA restriction) |
| “AP’s confirmation” | How GBP complies, citing SCs, PNs, and relevant-department agreement |
| Relevant departments | Use the list in LandsD’s post-sale/grant notification letter |
| Virtually unrestricted | Appendix I sample **does not apply** to PN 1/2017 redevelopment GBP |
| Status of schedule | Cursory summary only — **not** the lease; AP must supply further info on request |

**SD takeaway:** At SD, schedule every lease graphic (NBA, coloured formation areas, setback, access points) onto the Block Plan and quote **min/max GFA, SC, DDH, greenery, parking** against drawing numbers — LandsD will reject an incomplete schedule without reading the plans.

---

### Source

- `PN 2_2018.pdf` (LAO PN Issue No. 2/2018, 26 November 2018)
- Read with: **LAO PN 4/2018** (streamlined GBP / workshops); **LAO PN 3/2018** (computer GFA / coloured plans); **LAO PN 3/2014** (DDH); **LAO PN 4/2006** (parking schedule example in guidance); **LAO PN 1/2017** (virtually unrestricted exclusion)
*Source Critical Summary:* `source_md/LAO Practice Note No. 2_2018 (November 2018)_CS.md`

<a id="pn-3-2018"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 3/2018 — Computer calculation of areas / coloured building plans
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
26 November 2018 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Issue No. 3/2018 | Supersedes **LAO PN 8/2006** | Applies to new GBP (incl. subsequent amendments/major revisions/re-submissions) received via Centralized Processing System **on or after 1 February 2019**

> Scope note: Electronic **CAD** format and **Coloured Building Plans** so LandsD can check **lease** GFA/SC. Approval/disapproval under lease is still given on **hard copies** — DVD-ROM does not replace paper GBP. **Not** applicable as a DVD/CBP requirement to ordinary A&A (annotations/dimensions on hard copy suffice). Relates to **PNAP ADM-19** para 16 and Appendix F.

---

## Regulatory Overview

AP should follow Annex 1 for CAD on **non-rewritable DVD-ROM**, and Annex 3 for a full set of hard-copy floor plans identical to GBP layouts, coloured for SC, transfer plate, and GFA accountable/non-accountable **under lease**. Soft and hard copies **must be identical**. Practice to be reviewed **6 months** after implementation (including possible BIM for GFA).

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. When DVD-ROM / CBP are required — and reject clocks (paras 3–8)

| Item | Rule |
|---|---|
| Approval medium | Hard-copy GBP only |
| CBP vs GBP diagrams | Coloured extents must **match** GFA/SC calculations and diagrams in GBP; mismatch → reject without further checking |
| DVD not conforming (Annex 1 §§3.1–3.4) | LandsD requires replacement; **performance-pledge clock frozen**; replacement **within 7 calendar days** → clock resumes; **after 7 days** → clock **re-set** as new start; no replacement before pledge expiry → reject for insufficient information |
| Pre-1 Feb 2019 receipts | Continue under previous practice |
| A&A / addition / alteration | **No** new DVD-ROM or CBP required; if submitted, **not considered**; put annotations and dimensions on hard-copy GBP |

**SD takeaway:** Budget **hard-copy coloured plans + identical .dwg DVD** for first/major GBP from **1 February 2019**; do not treat A&A as a DVD job.

---

### 2. CAD minimums — reject without further checking (Annex 1)

| Topic | Hard rule |
|---|---|
| Media | Non-rewritable **DVD-ROM** only (unless Director agrees otherwise) |
| Format | **.dwg** only; AutoCAD **R14 or later** (or software accepted by Director); no zip/compress |
| Files | **One** hard-copy drawing per CAD file; default zoom full extent; all approval info in **same** file (cross-ref only if software layer limits; then path trial in hard copy; all files in **one folder**) |
| Covering letter | List drawing file name(s) used for lease area checking; **certify** GFA/SC computations and deductions are **directly from CAD with no manual input** — missing this → reject |
| Layers | Names per **Annex 2** (CSWP); non-conforming layer names → reject; extra layers allowed if legend + covering letter |
| Model space | True size, **1 drawing unit = 1 mm or metre**, precision nearest **mm**; LandsD default checker is **millimetre** |
| Polylines | **Closed** polylines; one closed polyline = one area diagram; open / self-intersecting / duplicated → reject |
| Dimensions | Software-generated true dimensions in Dimension Layer; **typed-in** dimensions → reject |
| Areas | **m²** to **3 decimal places** |
| Content | No lighting/appliances etc. not requiring Director’s approval under lease |
| If minima met | Diagrammatic GFA/SC/green-feature **breakdowns not required** on plans; **annotations and dimensions still required** on each floor plan |

**SD takeaway:** Draw lease GFA/SC as **closed mm polylines on Annex 2 layers** with auto dimensions and a covering-letter CAD-no-manual-input certificate — any typed dimension or open polyline is an automatic reject.

---

### 3. Layer families and coloured-plan colours (Annexes 2–3)

| Calculation family | Layer stem (ARC prefix ignored by LandsD checker) |
|---|---|
| Non-domestic SC | ADA08610 / 08615 + 0861A–Z deductions (JPN 1&2, curtain wall, E&M/refuse/APP-89 lifts, private parking/L/UL, misc.; skip I and O) |
| Domestic SC | ADA08620 / 08625 + 0862A–H then J–Z (adds caretaker office/quarters, rec facilities, OC office) |
| Non-domestic GFA | ADA08640 / 08645 + 0864A–K then L–Z (adds covered walkways, mail rooms/nests for **C/I** only, RCHE, GA, PTT, hotel BOH) |
| Domestic GFA | ADA08650 / 08655 + 0865A–M, P, then N–Z (adds bay windows, rec facilities under **PN 4/2000(B)**, logistic service room for **residential** only, rec-facility deduction layer) |
| NOFA | ADA08670 / 08675 |
| UFA / greenery | ADA08690 / 08695; 0869A–B obsolete rec under PN 4/2000; 0869C–H greenery / 50% green / green roof |
| Open space | ADA08600 / 08605 |
| Dimension | ADA086_8 |

**Standard CBP colours (RGB):** transfer plate = red thick dash (227,100,102); SC line = blue thick dash (73,167,209); building line above = black thick dash; residential = orange (255,164,25); balcony/UP edged orange; caretaker office = light brown hatched black; caretaker quarters = light brown; OC office = light brown cross-hatched; rec = pink (255,167,255); office = red hatched black; retail/commercial = red; hotel = light blue (144,214,236); GFA non-accountable **inside** accountable = blue; **outside** = yellow (255,237,61). Extra colours allowed (e.g. industrial, GA) if declared to LandsD. One hard copy, **same scale/size** as GBP floors.

**SD takeaway:** Colour **residential orange / rec pink / hotel light blue / deducted-in-GFA blue / deducted-outside yellow** on a full floor set that matches the CAD layers — LandsD checks colour vs numbers.

---

### Source

- `PN 3_2018.pdf` (LAO PN Issue No. 3/2018, 26 November 2018)
- Read with: **PNAP ADM-19** Appendix F; **LAO PN 2/2018** and **4/2018** (note 4/2018 Stage 1 says DVD-ROM **not** required at Stage 1); **LAO PN 4/2000(B)**; **PNAP APP-89**; **JPN Nos. 1 & 2**
*Source Critical Summary:* `source_md/LAO Practice Note No. 3_2018 (November 2018)_CS.md`

<a id="pn-4-2018"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 4/2018 — Streamlined building plans checking process
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
26 November 2018 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Issue No. 4/2018 | Streamlined Process applies to **new** GBP (incl. resubmissions / major revisions) received via Centralized Processing System **on or after 1 February 2019**

> Scope note: Three-stage **lease** GBP vetting plus optional **workshops**. LandsD will **not** check BO amendment submissions that have **no lease implications** (owner/AP must flag those that do). **Not** a substitute for BO approval. **Not** applicable to virtually unrestricted redevelopment under **PN 1/2017**, A&A to existing buildings, or technical plans (site formation, drainage, hoarding, green/yellow formation, landscape plan/LMP).

---

## Regulatory Overview

AP must ensure correctness of all arithmetic and area calculations (including GFA and SC) and demonstrate lease compliance in a **Development Schedule** with the GBP (**PN 2/2018**). “Major revision” (Director’s decision final) includes changes in disposition, number of blocks/storeys, floor-plan configuration; substantial changes in site area, formation levels and configuration; changes that seriously affect access. Pre-1 February 2019 receipts continue under previous practice.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. Three-stage GBP checking (para 4)

| Stage | When | What LandsD does | AP must submit |
|---|---|---|---|
| **1** — full GBP before commencement | First full set | **Does not** check area calculations; checks **other** lease conditions. May give **approval in-principle** if acceptable | Full GBP + Development Schedule with **AP’s Certification** of arithmetic/area correctness and lease compliance. If computer used for calcs: **one set Coloured Building Plans** (**PN 3/2018**). **DVD-ROM not required** at Stage 1 |
| **2** — pre-sale | **Upon or prior to** Pre-sale Consent application to **LACO** | Full check **including area calculations**. Approve/accept if lease-compliant **or** irregularities rectified | Certify BA-approved up-to-date GBP (drawing nos. + version date); submit that full set **direct to DLO**; comply with **PN 2/2018 and 3/2018** including **DVD-ROM**; certify it is the same set referred to in the Pre-sale Consent application |
| **3** — OP | **Upon or prior to** OP application to BA | Same full check as Stage 2 | Same as Stage 2, but certify the set is the one referred to BA for **OP** |

**In-principle (Stage 1) condition:** up-to-date BA-approved full GBP must later be submitted for lease approval at Stage 2 and/or 3 as applicable.

**SD takeaway:** Stage 1 is **in-principle on everything except numbers**; freeze GFA/SC for **DVD + coloured plans at Stage 2 (pre-sale) and Stage 3 (OP)** — do not start sales or OP on Stage 1 alone.

---

### 2. Errors, modification window, and Stage 3 hard stop (para 4(II)–(III))

| Item | Rule |
|---|---|
| Rectify | Owner must rectify (including demolition/removal and GBP amendments) to LandsD satisfaction; keep BA/other departments informed and get their approvals as needed |
| Stage 2 escape | If not rectifiable, owner **may apply** for approval, consent or **lease modification** to relax parameters **for the lifetime of the buildings** under the GBP — **no guarantee**; premium + admin fee as imposed; GBP not approved until terms accepted, paid, and legal document executed |
| Stage 3 | **No** approval/consent/modification to relax parameters or paper over irregularities **would normally be entertained**; rectify. Failure may lead to lease enforcement and **re-entry** |
| Processing time | Performance pledges apply to all three stages; clock from **valid** receipt (no outstanding AP information) |

**SD takeaway:** Any lease GFA/SC or bulk overrun must be **fixed or modified at Stage 2** — Stage 3 is a rectify-or-enforcement gate, not a premium negotiation.

---

### 3. Workshops (paras 6–8, 10)

| Item | Rule |
|---|---|
| Who | All development types (rationalised from comprehensive-development presentations) |
| How | Written request stating purpose (concept presentation **or** fundamental issues) + drawings; **no walk-ins** |
| Cap | Generally **maximum two workshops per site** before a GBP submission, resubmission or major revision |
| Alternative | DLO may divert to enquiry submission or other means |
| What it is **not** | Not confirmation of lease compliance; **will not** interpret lease terms; comments **without prejudice / no commitment** |
| What it may do | Elaborate prevailing **LAO PN** guidelines on plan checking |

**SD takeaway:** Use at most **two** pre-GBP workshops for interfaces (Government/public facilities, access, bulk) — treat every comment as non-binding.

---

### Source

- `PN 4_2018.pdf` (LAO PN Issue No. 4/2018, 26 November 2018)
- Read with: **LAO PN 2/2018** (Development Schedule); **LAO PN 3/2018** (DVD-ROM / CBP); **PNAP ADM-2** (Centralized Processing System); **LAO PN 1/2017** (excluded)
*Source Critical Summary:* `source_md/LAO Practice Note No. 4_2018 (November 2018)_CS.md`

---

<a id="pn-1-2019"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 1/2019 — Acceptable uses in ‘godown purposes’ premises
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
21 January 2019 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Issue No. 1/2019

> Scope note: Landlord view on whether **“cargo handling and forwarding facility”** and **“recyclable collection centre”** (TPB Definitions of Terms, with exclusions) may be accepted in premises restricted to **‘godown purposes’**. It does **not** interpret other users, waive lease, or replace FSD/BA licensing. Director’s decision on lease conformity is **final**.

---

## Regulatory Overview

Godown Premises may only be used for **storage of goods**, with **movement** of goods expected and the operation involving **transient deposit and storage for delivery**. LandsD will generally accept the two named uses if there is **primarily storage** of goods/material with those attendant consequences. An overall view of all activities (nature and cumulative effect), the particular lease, proposed operation design, operation details and technology employed will be considered case by case.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. What may be accepted — and what is excluded (paras 1–3, fn 2)

| Use (TPB DoT meaning, as adopted here) | Exclusion in this PN |
|---|---|
| **Cargo handling and forwarding facility** — inbound / transhipment / outbound cargo processing and/or freight forwarding, including logistics support such as packaging, labelling and final assembly for distribution | **Does not include** container freight station **or** free-standing purpose-designed logistics centre |
| **Recyclable collection centre** — community-based recycling: collection, storage, sorting, packing and baling of recyclable materials for recycling | **Does not include** sorting of **municipal solid waste** |
| Prerequisite | **Primarily storage** in the Godown Premises, with movement/transient deposit as in para 1 |

**SD takeaway:** Keep the floor as **storage-led** godown with cargo-handling or recyclable-collection ancillary to that storage — do not design a **CFS, purpose-built logistics centre, or MSW sorting hall** onto a godown user.

---

### Source

- `PN1_2019.pdf` (LAO PN Issue No. 1/2019, 21 January 2019)
- Read with: TPB **Definitions of Terms**; FSD / BA licences; **LAO PN 4/2019** Appendix II (standard-rate waiver list cross-refers this PN for godown vs industrial premises)
*Source Critical Summary:* `source_md/LAO Practice Note No. 1_2019 (January 2019)_CS.md`

---

<a id="pn-2-2019"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 2/2019 — Redevelopment of industrial lot — time-limited PR relaxation
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
18 February 2019 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Issue No. 2/2019

> Scope note: Special **time-limited** lease modification (or in-situ land exchange) for **pre-1987 industrial buildings** on industrial lots **outside “Residential” zone** in Main Urban Areas and New Towns, to take up TPB-approved **non-domestic plot ratio** of **not more than 20% above** the statutory-plan maximum (and not above **B(P)Reg** max non-domestic PR). It is **not** a wholesale-conversion waiver (see **PN 6/2019**) and not a standing open policy after the TPB window (later extended by **PN 2/2019A**).

---

## Regulatory Overview

A **pre-1987 IB** is wholly or partly constructed **on or before 1 March 1987**, or built to plans **first submitted** to BA **on or before 1 March 1987**. An **industrial lot** is restricted to industrial or godown or both; a pre-1987 IB on a grant **without** specific user restriction may still be treated as an industrial lot **for this PN**. **Main Urban Areas** (HKPSG): Hong Kong Island, Kowloon and New Kowloon, Tsuen Wan, Kwai Chung, Tsing Yi. **New Towns:** Sha Tin, Tai Po, Fanling/Sheung Shui, Tuen Mun, Yuen Long, Tin Shui Wai, Tseung Kwan O, Tung Chung.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. Planning window, lease timing, and building covenant (paras 2–4)

| Lock | Rule |
|---|---|
| TPB window (as issued) | **Three years commencing 10 October 2018** (varied by **PN 2/2019A** to **10 October 2018 – 31 October 2024** inclusive) |
| Eligible sites | Pre-1987 IBs on industrial lots **outside “R”** in Main Urban Areas and New Towns |
| PR cap | TPB-approved increase **must not** exceed max non-domestic PR under **Building (Planning) Regulations** |
| Execute modification | Lease modification letter **no later than three years** from date of **TPB approval letter**; **no EOT** unless LandsD finds strong and valid justifications (LandsD decision final) |
| Building covenant | Normally **complete redevelopment within five years** from execution of the modification letter |
| Premium | Terms including **premium** and admin fee as LandsD imposes; max intensity and modified BC specified in the letter |
| Admin fee | **Two instalments** (on demand at submission; balance on acceptance of binding basic terms) + LACO fee if applicable |

**SD takeaway:** Do not start lease-mod drafting until **TPB PR** is in hand; then execute within **3 years of the TPB letter** and programme completion within **5 years of execution**.

---

### 2. Filing, multiple ownership, and switch from an in-hand application (paras 6–10)

| Item | Rule |
|---|---|
| Submit to | Units 07–11, 27/F CDW Building, 382–392 Castle Peak Road, Tsuen Wan — **IBRU** |
| Multiple ownership | Appoint solicitors to arrange execution by **all interested parties** and confirm up-to-date ownership, unless LandsD dispenses |
| Original Application already in hand | After TPB approval, may file **New Application** and ask LandsD to **defer** Original; if New rejected/not pursued or not executed within the 3-year TPB clock, Original resumes unless withdrawn; if New executed in time, Original **deemed withdrawn** |
| Fees on switch | Original admin + LACO **not refunded**; New Application has its own fees |

**SD takeaway:** Multiple-ownership lots need a **single solicitor-led execution set**; switching onto this 20% track kills the old application’s fees.

---

### Source

- `PN 2_2019.pdf` (LAO PN Issue No. 2/2019, 18 February 2019)
- Read with: **LAO PN 2/2019A** (TPB window extended to 31 October 2024); **LAO PN 6/2019** para 17 (concurrent lease-mod under this PN **and** entire-IB special waiver **not accepted**); TPB / B(P)Reg
*Source Critical Summary:* `source_md/LAO Practice Note No. 2_2019 (February 2019)_CS.md`

## LAO Practice Note No. 2/2019A — Redevelopment of industrial lot — TPB window extension
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
7 October 2021 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Issue No. 2/2019A | Immediate effect | Varies/supplements **LAO PN 2/2019**

> Scope note: **Only** extends the Town Planning Board application period in PN 2/2019 para 2. All other 2/2019 provisions (3-year execution from TPB letter, 5-year building covenant, 20% non-domestic PR, outside-“R” geography, etc.) **remain valid**.

---

## Regulatory Overview

Words **“During the three-year period commencing from 10 October 2018”** in PN 2/2019 paragraph 2 are varied to **“From 10 October 2018 to 31 October 2024 (both dates inclusive)”**.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. The only change (para 2)

| Item | As varied |
|---|---|
| TPB filing window for redevelopment above statutory-plan max intensity | **10 October 2018 – 31 October 2024** (both dates inclusive) |

**SD takeaway:** The **20% non-domestic PR** lease-mod track remains available only if TPB is applied to **on or before 31 October 2024** — execution is still **within 3 years of the TPB approval letter** per parent PN 2/2019.

<a id="pn-3-2019"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 3/2019 — Waivers for buffer floor and lower floors of an existing IB
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
21 March 2019 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Issue No. 3/2019 | Submit to **IBRU**, Units 07–11, 27/F CDW Building, Tsuen Wan

> Scope note: Waiver for **temporary** change of use of a **buffer floor** (existing or to be converted) to **telecommunications exchange centre** and **computer or data processing centre**, together with **all floors immediately below** (max **three** lower floors) **en bloc** for **non-industrial** use — to meet **FSD Circular Letter No. 2/2019**. It is **not** a lifetime waiver under this PN. Not a BO GFA bonus route. Director of Fire Services decides what constitutes the buffer floor and lower three floors.

---

## Regulatory Overview

Buffer floors may be required to separate non-industrial uses (which themselves need waiver/modification) on lower floors from industrial uses above. Prevailing FSD practice allowed buffer floors for parking, L/UL, E&M plant, or vacant refuge. This PN widens buffer-floor user. Application only if OZP permits (or TPB permission exists), FSD CL 2/2019 is met, and the Premises are under **single ownership** (same registered owner for buffer **and** the lower floors in the application).

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. Eligible uses and package (paras 1–3, fn 3–6)

| Lock | Rule |
|---|---|
| Buffer-floor user | **Telecommunications exchange centre** and **computer or data processing centre** only (Director of Lands’ determination final) |
| Lower floors | Waiver for buffer **must include** conversion of **all** floors immediately below, **subject to a maximum of three lower floors**, for **non-industrial** uses (FSD qualifying requirements; D of FS **final**) |
| Ownership | Same person/entity owns buffer **and** those lower floors |
| Not included on buffer | **No** customer services or sale of goods (whether public attends or not); **no** general commercial/office merely because they use computers/telecom equipment |
| Telecom exchange | Place where exchange equipment is installed for **public** telecom (fixed/mobile telephone and data). LandsD determination final |
| Computer / data processing centre | Computing/data processing with a **functional relationship with business activities**; housing computers/servers/telecom + support E&M in a secure controlled environment. LandsD determination final |

**SD takeaway:** Convert **buffer + up to three floors below as one ownership package**; put **data/telecom plant on the buffer** — not a shop, counter, or generic office.

---

### 2. GFA/PR, term, fees, and fire (paras 4–7)

| Lock | Rule |
|---|---|
| Not lifetime | Waiver **will not** be granted for the lifetime of the existing building **under this PN** |
| Personal | **Personal to the owner**; **deemed terminated** on change of ownership of the Premises or any part |
| User during waiver | No part of Premises for uses other than those specified |
| Plot ratio | After conversion, IB PR must **not exceed** max intensity under the **BO** |
| GFA / SC | Total GFA or SC of the Premises **after** conversion (BO or lease, as the case may be) **shall not exceed** total GFA or SC of the Premises **before** conversion on the **latest BA- and LandsD-approved** plans. **No additional or bonus GFA** from BO (or otherwise) due to conversion |
| Works / FSD | Conversion and Premises must comply with **FSD Circular Letter No. 2/2019** (or amending FSD documents) |
| Fees | Admin fee on demand (**non-refundable**); waiver fee **and deposit** as imposed. Telecom-exchange fee: **PN 5/2001 & 5/2001A** as varied by **PN 1/2015**, **except** standard lump-sum lifetime rates. Computer/data centre fee: **PN 3/2012** (or amending PNs). Other uses: established practice |
| Other licences | FSD and BA still required; waiver is **not** a feasibility guarantee |

**SD takeaway:** Keep converted GFA/SC **≤ pre-conversion approved totals** with **zero bonus GFA**, and design to **FSD CL 2/2019** — this waiver is **temporary and personal**, not a building-life conversion.

---

### 3. Application pack highlights (Appendix I checklist)

| Must | Detail |
|---|---|
| Latest approved plans | 2 copies + schedule of **total GFA and total IFA** of the Premises (AP-certified) |
| OPs | 2 copies, AP-certified |
| Floor plans | **4 copies**, ≤ A3, Premises edged **pink**, IFA with use breakdown |
| Data-centre concept | If computer/data processing on buffer — conceptual design per fn 4; **energy saving plan** (OGCIO Green Data Centre Practices) |
| Works proposal | Certify any change in GFA, SC, storeys, height; **FSD CL 2/2019** compliance; no other lease non-compliance |
| Existing waiver | Replaced on execution; **no** fee refund |

**SD takeaway:** Issue pink-edged IFA plans and an FSD CL 2/2019 / GFA-no-increase certificate with the waiver — bonus PR from conversion is off the table.

---

- Read with: **FSD Circular Letter No. 2/2019**; **LAO PN 3/2012** (data-centre waiver fee); **LAO PN 5/2001, 5/2001A, 1/2015** (telecom-exchange fee); OGCIO Green Data Centre Practices
*Source Critical Summary:* `source_md/LAO Practice Note No. 3_2019 (March 2019)_CS.md`

<a id="pn-4-2019"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 4/2019 — Waivers for industrial premises (non-data-centre / non-testing-lab)
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
22 March 2019 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Issue No. 4/2019 | From **1 April 2019** | Target issue **≤ 2 months** from valid receipt | Supersedes **PN 5/2001, 5/2001A, 2/2003 and 1/2015** together with **PN 5/2019**

> Scope note: Streamlined **temporary waivers** for **non-industrial** use of existing **Industrial Premises** (part(s) of an IB on an industrial or industrial-and/or-godown lease). **Does not** cover data-centre waivers (**PN 3/2012 series**) or testing-laboratory waivers (**PN 1/2016**). Rooftop/external **radio base stations** → **PN 5/2019**. Not a planning permission.

---

## Regulatory Overview

Industrial Premises may only be used for purposes involving a **manufacturing process**. **Technology-based crop and aquaculture production** (controlled-environment production of crops/aquatic organisms; no direct customer services or goods) is **generally accepted as industrial** under lease. Standard-rate Schedule-of-Uses procedure **does not apply** to premises on **basement, ground or podium floors**, or to buildings with BA plans approved **on or after 1 April 2007** (or earlier plans with **major amendments** on or after that date) — those fees are individually assessed. Director’s decisions on user, floor location and major/minor amendments are **final**.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. Two waiver tracks (paras 2–5, Appendix II)

| Track | Permitted uses | Fee basis |
|---|---|---|
| **Schedule of Permitted Uses** (Appendix II) | (1) HQ / back-office; (2) professional consultants’ offices (architects, engineers, surveyors, planning consultants, solicitors, accountants); (3) business-services offices (advertising, management consultants, PR, interior/graphic designers); (4) IT & telecom industries (see track 2 list; **excludes** rooftop/external RBS per PN 5/2019); (5) cargo handling and forwarding facilities; (6) recyclable collection centres; (7) other **non-residential** uses **not** involving **direct provision of customer services or goods to the general public** | Appendix III standard rates |
| **IT & telecom exclusive** (para 4) | Manufacture, design, development, production, operation, processing, assembly or **research** into: (i) electronic/micro-electronic systems, goods, components; (ii) IT products/services (computer HW/SW, contents, applications); (iii) telecom facilities/services as in **Cap. 106** — plus **ancillary office and storage** directly related. **No** customer/retail services with **physical attendance** of customers/public. Illustrations: gateways, exchanges, switching centres, RBS, paging centres, computer/data centres, telesites/carrier hotels. **Excludes** general commercial/office (trading, accounting, architecture, engineering consultancy, travel, estate agents, employment agencies, financial consultants, brokers, etc.) | Appendix IV standard rates |

**Godown vs industrial (App II fn 3):** Cargo handling / recyclable collection as in **PN 1/2019** on **godown-user** premises are generally accepted **without** this waiver. Those uses here **exclude** CFS / purpose-designed logistics centre and **MSW sorting**.

**SD takeaway:** Stay off **G/F, basement and podium** if you want standard rates; keep the user as **back-office / professional / IT-telecom / no-public-counter** — a shop or public-facing service is outside this PN’s Schedule.

---

### 2. Standard waiver fees from 1 April 2019 (Appendices III–IV)

**Appendix III — Schedule uses (not G/F, basement, podium; not post-1 Apr 2007 BA plans / major amendments)**

| Location | Annual fee (IFA) | Lump sum for **lifetime of building** (IFA) |
|---|---|---|
| Urban Area and Tsuen Wan / Kwai Tsing | **$718/m² p.a.** | **$6,540/m²** |
| NT other than Tsuen Wan / Kwai Tsing | **$549/m² p.a.** | **$4,980/m²** |

**Appendix IV — IT & telecom exclusive (RBS outside building/rooftop excluded)**

| DLO districts | Annual (IFA) | Lifetime lump sum (IFA) |
|---|---|---|
| HK West & South, HK East, Kowloon East, Kowloon West, Tsuen Wan and Kwai Tsing | **$104/m² p.a.** | **$920/m²** |
| Tuen Mun, Yuen Long, North, Tai Po, Shatin, Sai Kung, Islands | **$69/m² p.a.** | **$610/m²** |

**IFA:** enclosed internal space for exclusive use including balconies, toilets and lift lobbies forming part of the unit; measured to interior face of external/separating wall; **include** internal partitions and columns.

Application may be for an initial term of years **or** lifetime of the existing building. Admin fee on demand, non-refundable. TPB / FSD / BA still required.

**SD takeaway:** Price the waiver on **IFA** (not GFA) and pick **annual vs lifetime lump sum** at SD; post-2007 IBs and G/F units are **individually assessed**, not these tables.

---

### Source

- `PN 4_2019.pdf` (LAO PN Issue No. 4/2019, 22 March 2019)
- Read with: **LAO PN 3/2012, 3/2012A, 3/2012B** (data centre); **LAO PN 1/2016** (testing lab); **LAO PN 5/2019** (RBS); **LAO PN 1/2019** (godown cargo/recycling)
*Source Critical Summary:* `source_md/LAO Practice Note No. 4_2019 (March 2019)_CS.md`

<a id="pn-5-2019"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 5/2019 — Waivers for radio base stations for mobile services
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
22 March 2019 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Issue No. 5/2019 | From **1 April 2019** | Target issue **≤ 2 months** from valid receipt | Together with **PN 4/2019**, supersedes **PN 9/2002, 3/2013 and 1/2015** from 1 April 2019

> Scope note: Temporary waiver for **radio base stations and associated equipment/facilities** in **existing buildings** where installation would breach lease **user** (installations treated as **commercial**). Waiver may also cover consequential breaches such as **GFA and height**. **Inside industrial buildings**, fees follow **PN 4/2019 Appendix IV** (IT & telecom rates), not Appendix III of this PN. Not a CA licence and not planning permission.

---

## Regulatory Overview

CA-licensed mobile network operators may need to install RBS in existing buildings. On lots held for **private residential** or other **non-commercial** purposes, RBS is a user breach. Applications go to the **relevant DLO**. Admin fee on demand is non-refundable. Waiver is temporary user variation only; TPB, FSD and BA remain applicable.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. When a waiver is needed and what it covers (paras 2–5)

| Item | Rule |
|---|---|
| Trigger | RBS + associated equipment/facilities in buildings on **private residential** or other **non-commercial** leases (commercial nature) |
| Scope of waiver | User **and**, as the case may be, other consequential breaches (**GFA, height**, etc.) |
| IB interior | Fee = **PN 4/2019 Appendix IV** standard rates (subject to review) |
| Other existing buildings | Fee = **this PN Appendix III** |
| Term in sample form | Initially a stated number of **years** |
| Drawings required | Size/dimensions of equipment room/shelter; area covered by equipment/installations (whether housed or not); area of **base** supporting them; **number of antennae**. Premises include **rooftops and external walls** |

**SD takeaway:** Treat rooftop/external-wall RBS as a **lease GFA/height** issue as well as user — survey antenna count and equipment-base area for the waiver drawings, not only the equipment room.

---

### 2. Standard annual fees from 1 April 2019 (Appendix III) — non-IB

| Component | Fee |
|---|---|
| Station area **not exceeding 5 m²** with **5 antennae** | **$79,800 p.a.** |
| Each **additional antenna** | **$15,300 p.a.** |
| **Additional area** | **$1,350 / m² p.a.** |

Rates subject to review. IB interiors use PN 4/2019 App IV instead.

**SD takeaway:** Keep the station **≤ 5 m² and ≤ 5 antennae** for the base rate; extra dishes and extra area are priced **per antenna / per m²**.

---

### Source

- `PN 5_2019.pdf` (LAO PN Issue No. 5/2019, 22 March 2019)
- Read with: **LAO PN 4/2019** Appendix IV (IB interior RBS fees); Communications Authority licences; TPB / FSD / BA
*Source Critical Summary:* `source_md/LAO Practice Note No. 5_2019 (March 2019)_CS.md`

<a id="pn-6-2019"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 6/2019 — Special waiver for conversion of an entire existing industrial building
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
2 April 2019 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Issue No. 6/2019 | Valid applications received **on or before 31 March 2022** (extended to **31 October 2024** by **PN 6/2019A**)

> Scope note: **Nil waiver fee** special waiver to change use of an **entire existing IB** in **“I” / “C” / “OU(B)”** for the building’s lifetime or lease expiry/termination, whichever earlier. Requires a **≥10% Designated Portion** for Government-specified uses, **no GFA increase**, tightly limited envelope changes, **3-year** conversion completion, and **Director’s consent** to dispose (via **PN 2/2016**). **Not** concurrent with **PN 2/2019** lease modification. Vacant-site redevelopment is outside this PN.

---

## Regulatory Overview

Building age **≥ 15 years** from OP of the **entire** IB (if multiple OPs, from the **latest**). Application by lot owner, or **jointly by all** existing owners plus consent of all mortgagees/chargees/ASP purchasers. Proposed uses must be always-permitted on the OZP **or** have **TPB permission before** LandsD filing. If the zone leaves the Designated Zones **before execution**, the application is **rejected** (admin fee refundable at LandsD discretion). Special factories/lots excluded as in other IB PNs.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. Designated Portion — 10% specified uses (para 3(a), 4)

| Lock | Rule |
|---|---|
| Quantum | **Not less than 10%** of total **accountable GFA under the BO** of the existing IB **after conversion** |
| Uses | **Specified Uses** as Government determines, consistent with para 2(c) town planning; owners may propose GFA, location, layout, FF&E (suggested: arts/culture, creative industries, I&T, social service/community healthcare, sports/recreation, or other wider community benefits — e.g. small studios, young-entrepreneur workshops, I&T incubation, elderly service centres). If owners submit no preference, **Government decides** (final) |
| Commence and continue | Start using Designated Portion for Specified Uses within **6 calendar months** of conversion completion; continue throughout the waiver to policy bureaux/departments’ satisfaction |
| Cessation | Unused or inadequately used for **6 calendar months** = breach of commence-and-continue |
| Alienation | **No** assign, mortgage, charge, demise, part with possession, licence or right to occupy the Designated Portion, except the **Long Lease** below |
| Breach remedy | Government may require owner, at own cost, to underlet Designated Portion (or part) to **FSI** at **$1** p.a. if demanded, for residue of waiver period, and deliver **vacant possession** free of incumbrances |
| Cost | All wholesale-conversion costs of Designated Portion borne by owners; tenancy rent is owner–tenant (rental concession welcomed but not prescribed) |

**SD takeaway:** Reserve **≥ 10% BO accountable GFA** as a Government-controlled community/I&T/arts package that **cannot be sold** — locate it as a discrete, operable floor plate before conversion drawings.

---

### 2. Envelope, GFA, height, and works (para 3(b)–(c), 5)

| Lock | Rule |
|---|---|
| GFA cap | Total accountable GFA on **Last Approved Building Plans** (BA, before application) **must not be exceeded**; **no** BO additional SC or PR concession |
| External change generally | **No** change to external structures, **no** increase in height or bulk, **except**: |
| (I) Demolish GFA-accountable external structures | Up to **10%** of total accountable GFA on last approved plans |
| (II) Recover demolished GFA | Vertical and/or horizontal extensions **within lot** if: converted building SC **≤ BO** SC; and BH and/or SC **≤ OZP** (or TPB minor relaxation). **Does not** cover internal works that do not change the envelope; internal conversion within envelope remains permitted if no BH/bulk increase and GFA cap kept |
| (III) Roof-top plant | Machine rooms, A/C units, water tanks, stairhoods and similar on **main roof**, **BO GFA-exempt**, occupying **not more than 50%** of roof area of floor immediately below |
| (IV) Cladding / curtain wall | Added to outer face **within lot**, BO GFA-exempt. If they **protrude onto Government land**, LandsD **may** approve a modification with admin fee and **premium waived** |
| Lease BH | Must be observed, **save** LandsD **may** waive BH non-compliance in the special waiver (nil fee) **solely** for GFA recovery under (II) and/or rooftop structures under (III) |
| Other lease breaches | If proposal breaches lease **other than** user (or BH to the extent permitted under (II)/(III)), a **separate lease modification** (premium as appropriate) must be **completed before** the special waiver is executed |
| Completion | Conversion works BO-compliant; finish within **3 years** of special waiver letter; AP (or LandsD-accepted professional) certified proof (e.g. Form **BA 14** or works-contract completion) |

**SD takeaway:** Conversion is **GFA-neutral** on last-approved totals; you may **carve off ≤ 10% GFA** of accountable external structure and **rebuild it as vertical/horizontal extension** only if OZP BH/SC and BO SC still hold.

---

### 3. Disposal, user, existing waivers, and conflicts (paras 3(d)–(e), 6–17)

| Item | Rule |
|---|---|
| Disposal | Except Designated Portion, no sale/assignment/agreement without **Director’s prior written consent** — follow **PN 2/2016** |
| User | No part of building for purposes other than those specified in the special waiver |
| After expiry | Lot reverts to original lease user **and height** (if any); surviving non-compliant uses/structures need a **modification** |
| Full-market premium | Any later modification assessed **without** benefit of the special waiver |
| Multiple ownership | Joint execution; solicitors confirm ownership |
| Existing unit waivers | Terminated on execution; **no** refund |
| Concurrent **PN 2/2019** | Lease-mod under 2/2019 **and** this special waiver **will not be accepted** concurrently |
| Revert | Cancel special waiver (all owners + consents if multiple ownership) to return to original lease uses not specified in the waiver |

**SD takeaway:** Choose **wholesale conversion (this PN)** or **20% PR redevelopment (PN 2/2019)** — not both — and programme **3-year** works plus **PN 2/2016** disposal consents for every unit sale.

---

### Source

- `PN 6_2019.pdf` (LAO PN Issue No. 6/2019, 2 April 2019)
- Read with: **LAO PN 6/2019A** (window to 31 October 2024); **LAO PN 2/2016** (disposal consent); **LAO PN 2/2019** (no concurrent application); **LAO PN 7/2019** (transitional housing overlay); OZP / TPB
*Source Critical Summary:* `source_md/LAO Practice Note No. 6_2019 (April 2019)_CS.md`

Issue No. 6/2019A | Immediate effect | Varies/supplements **LAO PN 6/2019**

## Critical main topics and subtopics
