<a id="pn-7-2019"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 7/2019 — Transitional housing in converted existing industrial buildings
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
18 April 2019 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Issue No. 7/2019 | Supplements **LAO PN 1/2010, 1/2010A, 1/2010B** (as supplemented by **PN 2/2016**) and **LAO PN 6/2019**

> Scope note: Overlay waiver for **transitional housing** in a converted existing IB (as part of, in addition to, or otherwise than the **Special Waiver** for entire-IB non-industrial conversion). **New** Special Waiver applications under the **1/2010** series are **not** accepted. Not a BO domestic conversion code and not a planning permission.

---

## Regulatory Overview

Owners may apply for a Transitional Housing Waiver for conversion and use of the converted IB **or part thereof** for transitional housing, for a term of **not more than five (5) years**, if the building is in **“Commercial”**, **“Comprehensive Development Area”**, **“OU(B)”**, or **“Residential”** on statutory plans, the use is permitted as temporary use under those plans, and the project is **NGO-run** and **supported by THB** (Task Force on Transitional Housing as one-stop facilitation).

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. Eligibility, term, and fees (paras 2–3)

| Lock | Rule |
|---|---|
| Operator | **Non-profit making organisation** or **social enterprise** (definitions in fn 1–2: s.88 charity; guarantee company without profit distribution; or other non-profit body under HK legislation; SE expected on HAD “Hot spots for consumptions at SEs” or SEBC Social Enterprise Directory) |
| Support | Waiver application **supported by THB**; Task Force provides one-stop facilitation |
| Zoning | **C / CDA / OU(B) / R** on TPO statutory plans; temporary transitional housing permitted (owners pointed to TPB press release **26 November 2018**) |
| Term | **Not more than 5 years** |
| Fees | If approved: **nil waiver fee**, **nil deposit**, and **nil or no additional** admin fee (as the case may be) |
| Other terms | As LandsD imposes (including Task Force recommendations), e.g. restriction on letting the wholesale-converted IB or part **to NGOs agreed by THB** |
| 1/2010 pipeline | No **new** Special Waiver under 1/2010 PNs. Owners **may** file a **supplementary** Transitional Housing Waiver where a 1/2010 Special Waiver is **under processing**, or a **new** Transitional Housing Waiver for a building **already** covered by a 1/2010 Special Waiver |
| Form | If not part of the original Special Waiver application, use **PN 6/2019 Appendices I and II** |

**SD takeaway:** Design transitional housing as a **≤ 5-year NGO operation** on **C / CDA / OU(B) / R** with **THB Task Force** backing — LandsD will not open a new 1/2010 wholesale-conversion track for it.

---

### 2. Other authorities (para 4)

| Item | Rule |
|---|---|
| Still required | TPB, FSD, BA and other licences whether or not works are involved |
| Not a guarantee | Issue of the waiver is not LandsD feasibility or cost acceptance |

**SD takeaway:** Run **FSD/BA/TPB** in parallel with the waiver — nil-fee LandsD consent does not clear fire or building conversion.

---

### Source

- `PN7_2019.pdf` (LAO PN Issue No. 7/2019, 18 April 2019)
- Read with: **LAO PN 6/2019** (and **6/2019A**); **LAO PN 1/2010, 1/2010A, 1/2010B** and **2/2016** (existing Special Waivers only); THB Task Force on Transitional Housing; TPB 26 November 2018 press release on eligible transitional housing as temporary use

<a id="pn-1-2020"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 1/2020 — Compliance of landscape clause under lease
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
January 2020 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Implements **JPN No. 3** (April 2019) | **Supersedes LAO PN No. 6/2003**  
Forms 1–5 and Figures 1–2 later superseded by **PN 1/2020A**

> Scope note: Landscape **submission and completion** for works **inside the private lot**. **Does not apply** to landscape for areas handed back to Government or **Coloured Areas** outside the lot. Deemed LandsD approval is **not** a waiver of other lease conditions.

---

## Regulatory Overview

“Existing lease” here means the landscape clause names **Director of Lands** as approving authority; “new lease” typically names **Director of Planning** or another B/D. TPRP is part and parcel of the landscape submission — use **LAO PN No. 2/2020** for the tree-preservation clause.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. What clause will appear in a new or modified lease (paras 4–5)

| Situation | Lease / process lock |
|---|---|
| PlanD or other B/D requests landscape with stated objectives | **LMP clause or LP clause**; that B/D is named as authority for submission **and** implementation (JPN 3). **No separate** landscape submission to LandsD |
| No specific landscape request | **Simple** clause: landscape and plant trees/shrubs on any part of the lot **not built upon** and maintain. **No** landscape submission or compliance checking |

**SD takeaway:** Push at land-grant stage for a **simple plant-and-maintain** clause unless PlanD/B/D actually wants an LMP/LP — that is the difference between a full landscape package and none.

---

### 2. Landscape tied to a planning application (paras 6–8)

PlanD checks submission and implementation (JPN 3 paras 3–4). **No separate** LandsD landscape submission.

| Stage | Lock |
|---|---|
| Submission | PlanD-approved LMP/LP + **Form 1** from lot owner or **RLA** → LandsD acknowledgement within **10 working days** = **deemed approval** of the lease landscape **submission**. LandsD will not consider Form 1 until PlanD has approved |
| Completion | PlanD-confirmed implementation + **Form 2** → same **10 working days** deemed **compliance** |
| Lot vs planning site | Form 1 requires stating whether lease lot boundary is **the same as or different from** the planning application site |
| SCG on lease | Greenery provision/calculation must be **consistent with BD-accepted GBPs**; attach BD SCG approval letter |
| TPRP | If trees (including OVTs / trees of particular value / lease-designated trees) are involved, deemed TPRP approval follows **PN 2/2020** |
| Class B LMP/LP amendments | PlanD **PNPP No. 1/2019** para 10 |
| Coloured Areas / other B/D landscape | That B/D checks both stages (lease, JPN 3, **DEVB TC(W) No. 6/2015**) |

**SD takeaway:** Align lot boundary, PlanD LMP/LP, BD **APP-152** greenery, and TPRP as one pack — LandsD only rubber-stamps after PlanD (and BD SCG if leased).

---

### 3. Landscape to satisfy lease only (no planning submission) (paras 9–12)

LandsD checks both stages unless the lease names PlanD/another B/D (JPN 3 paras 5(a)–(b)).

| Item | LMP clause | LP clause |
|---|---|---|
| Who prepares | **RLA mandatory** | RLA **optional** |
| Processing | LandsD within **8 weeks** of a valid submission | Same |
| Amendments | Submit updated LMP/LP with changes **highlighted** | Same |
| Completion SCC | **Mandatory** RLA: Form 3 + dated colour photos + key plan + **Form 4** SCC + **Form 5** owner’s statement | SCC **optional**; without SCC, DLO does **on-site full check**. If RLA is used, LMP SCC procedures apply |

**LMP completion / random audit (para 11):**

| Event | Clock |
|---|---|
| Minor as-built deviations allowed in SCC | More trees preserved/planted; minor planting-location or compensatory size/species changes; **no diminishing effect** — highlight in colour on approved LMP |
| Random full check | RLA told within **14 working days** of SCC; **silence = deemed complied** |
| If selected | Site check date within those 14 working days; result within **6 weeks after** on-site check |
| Rectification | Fresh SCC after all rectification |
| Clean check | DLO letter confirming landscape clause complied |

**SD takeaway:** Lease-only LMP needs an RLA from SD and an 8-week LandsD approval; completion SCC is mandatory and “more trees / minor moves” is the only as-built tolerance without a formal amendment.

---

### 4. Site coverage of greenery (para 14)

Follow **JPN 3 paras 9–11**. Landscape submission must show **location, disposition and layout** of greenery areas and greening features per **PNAP APP-152 Appendix D**.

**SD takeaway:** Lease SCG is the same drawing set as BD SBD greenery — do not run two layouts.

---

### 5. Workshop with GBP (para 15)

**LAO PN No. 4/2018** workshop applies to associated LMP/LP. AP and RLA must state purposes (landscape vs building layout, GBP/LMP issues) and attach drawings. BPU Landscape Advisory Team may join.

**SD takeaway:** Book a PN 4/2018 workshop when podium greenery fights GBP/SCG, not after LandsD rejection.

---

### Source

- `PN 1_2020.pdf`
- Varied by: **LAO PN No. 1/2020A** (SCC at submission; shorter completion clocks; Forms 1A–7). Read with: **JPN 3**; **PNAP APP-152**; **LAO PN Nos. 2/2020, 4/2018**; **DEVB TC(W) 6/2015**; PlanD **PNPP 1/2019**
*Source Critical Summary:* `source_md/LAO Practice Note No. 1-2020 (January 2020)_CS.md`

---

<a id="pn-1-2020a"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 1/2020A — Landscape clause: SCC at submission and faster completion checks
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
May 2022 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Immediate effect | Varies **LAO PN No. 1/2020**

> Scope note: Adds optional **self-certification at LMP/LP submission** (lease-only cases), early vetting during a binding land transaction, and **shorter** completion-audit clocks. It does **not** change JPN 3 planning-tied deemed approval, the simple plant-and-maintain clause, or Coloured-Area carve-out in PN 1/2020. Forms **1–5** and Figures **1–2** of PN 1/2020 are **superseded**.

---

## Regulatory Overview

Except as varied here, PN 1/2020 remains valid. “DLO” includes Railway Development Section and other specialised LandsD sections. Deemed approval/compliance still does not certify other lease conditions and may be **withdrawn or revoked** if the LMP/LP or SCC fails LandsD requirements.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. Optional SCC at submission — lease-only LMP/LP (paras 2–3; new Forms 6–7)

Lot owner **may** appoint an RLA to submit **Form 6** SCC + **Form 7** Owner’s Statement with the LMP/LP when **all** of the following are met:

| Condition | Lock |
|---|---|
| (a) | LMP/LP prepared by an **RLA** |
| (b) | LMP/LP incorporates the lot’s **latest TPRP approved by LandsD** |
| (c) | LMP/LP includes location, disposition and layout of greenery / greening features per **PNAP APP-152 Appendix D**, meeting the lease **prescriptive SCG** and **accepted by BD** |

| Audit path | Clock from valid LMP/LP + SCC receipt |
|---|---|
| Enter register; random full check with BPU **LAT** | RLA told within **10 working days** whether selected |
| **Not** selected | Acknowledgement = **deemed approval** of landscape **submission** within **4 weeks** |
| Selected | Result within **8 weeks**; if unacceptable, revise and re-submit |

**SD takeaway:** To use 4-week deemed LMP approval, freeze **LandsD-approved TPRP** and **BD-accepted SCG layout** into the same RLA drawings before lodging.

---

### 2. Early landscape vetting during land transaction (para 4)

If a land exchange / grant / modification is in process **and** the owner has entered a **binding contract and paid the required deposit**, owner or RLA may submit LMP/LP for vetting on the established timeframe. **Formal approval** is given only **after** the land transaction completes, and only if the submission is acceptable and PN 1/2020 criteria are met.

**SD takeaway:** Start LMP after PBTO/BBTO deposit — comments can flow, but do not treat them as lease approval until the new grant exists.

---

### 3. LAT comments (para 5)

LAT technical comments will be sent **to the RLA direct**, in advance of DLO’s consolidated reply.

**SD takeaway:** Route landscape queries to the RLA, not only the AP, so LAT notes are not sitting in a DLO letter.

---

### 4. Completion-stage clocks — revised para 11(c)–(d) of PN 1/2020 (para 6)

| Event | PN 1/2020 | This PN |
|---|---|---|
| Notice of random full check (LMP SCC) | 14 working days | **10 working days** from valid completion SCC; silence = deemed complied |
| Result if selected | Liaise within 14 working days; result **6 weeks after on-site check** | Result within **8 weeks from receipt** of completion SCC |
| Rectification + fresh SCC | Unchanged | Unchanged |

**SD takeaway:** Programme landscape CC around **10 working days / 8 weeks from SCC receipt**, not the old 14-day / 6-weeks-after-inspection pair.

---

### 5. Forms and flow charts (para 9)

| Superseded (PN 1/2020) | Replacement |
|---|---|
| Forms 1–5 | **1A, 2A, 3A, 4A, 5A** |
| Figures 1 and 2 | **Figure 1A** (submission), **Figure 1B** (submission with SCC), **Figure 2A** (completion) |
| New | **Form 6** (SCC on submission), **Form 7** (owner’s statement on submission SCC) |

Form 1A notes (design locks unchanged in substance): PlanD approval required before deemed-approval application; lot vs planning boundary; SCG vs BD plans; TPRP via **PN 2/2020**; Coloured Areas / other B/D still follow JPN 3 and **DEVB TC(W) No. 6/2015**.

**SD takeaway:** Use the May 2022 **A-series** forms and Figure 1B if submitting with SCC — old PN 1/2020 forms are dead.

---

### Source

- `PN 1_2020A.pdf`
- Read with: **LAO PN No. 1/2020**; **JPN 3**; **PNAP APP-152**; **LAO PN No. 2/2020**
*Source Critical Summary:* `source_md/LAO Practice Note No. 1-2020A (May 2022)_CS.md`

<a id="pn-3-2020"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 3/2020 — Design, Disposition and Height (DDH / DD) under Lease
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
27 April 2020 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Supersedes LAO PN 3/2014 | Read with **JPN 5** (building height) and LAO PN **4/2018** (streamlined GBP)

> Scope note: When **DDH or DD** is imposed, when it can be **omitted or stripped**, and **what LandsD actually checks** (site coverage, height, stilts, soil fill, parking headroom, transfer plates, walls, projections). **Pure aesthetics are generally not controlled.** It is **not** the BO SC/height code — BA is followed where stated. PN **3/2020A** later streamlines **SC** under this PN.

## Regulatory Overview

New/modified leases generally **omit building-height restriction** except special cases (**JPN 5**). A **DD** clause is also **generally not imposed** for the site types in para. 3 if the lease already controls essential parameters (GFA/PR). Existing DDH/DD can be **removed on modification** (new max GFA if missing, SBD, parking, user, landscape/trees, BC, premium). Where DDH/DD remains, approval is given **with GBP Stage 1** under PN 4/2018 — **no separate DDH application**.

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. When DD is generally **not** imposed (paras. 2–4)

DD generally **not** imposed (any site size) if the site is:

- **commercial**; or
- **private residential or composite C/R in Density Zone I (urban)**; or
- **R(A)** high-density / composite in the **NT**; or
- **industrial and/or godown**; or
- requires **TPB MLP/LP**, or planning condition for MLP/LP to PlanD, or **lease MLP/LP** per OZP ES;

**and** the lease has specific GFA/PR (etc.) control.

Urban-design / **flat-size or unit-number** controls → **specific SCs**, not a vague DD clause.

**SD takeaway:** On a new grant in those categories, do **not** design around a DDH negotiation — height lives in **JPN 5 / the OZP**, bulk in the **GFA SC**.

### 2. Stripping DDH/DD from an existing lease (para. 5)

On redevelopment modification, owner may apply to **remove** DDH/DD if the lot falls in para. 3(a)–(e). Approval typically adds **max GFA** (if none), SBD, parking, user, landscape/trees, BC, other bureau requirements, **admin fee + premium**.

**SD takeaway:** Budget a **GFA cap + SBD + parking** package as the price of deleting DDH — not a free deletion.

### 3. Site coverage under DDH (para. 7(a)) — later varied by 3/2020A

| Rule | Detail |
|---|---|
| Accountable | **All** building components on the lot, including **projections** and **underground structures outside footprint** (water tanks, plant rooms), unless **expressly exempted** under lease |
| Disregarded | Architectural features, cladding, minor features from the building face with **no significant bulk impact**, if BA disregards them **without** s.42 modification |
| Modern SC clause “at or above ground” | Does **not** catch **underground** buildings/structures |
| JPN 1/2 features | Balconies, UP, NSPE walls **SC-accountable** unless BA-exempt **and** lease exemption (those JPNs) applies |
| Curtain wall | SC-accountable unless BA-exempt **and** lease has the curtain-wall GFA/SC definition clause; if no exemption SC → **modification** after BA exemption |

**SD takeaway:** Assume **basements, tanks and plant outside the tower** count for lease SC unless the SC clause is ground-up only or an express exemption exists.

### 4. Building height (para. 7(b)) — follow JPN 5

| Scenario | How height is read |
|---|---|
| Default | **JPN 5** (from 15 May 2019) |
| JPN 5 does not apply (lease has different specs, or **no** development-intensity provisions) | Height from **lowest formation level** of any part of the building (**including basement and stilts**) to **top of highest main-roof slab** |
| Not counted as lowest level | Localised sunken slabs: lift pits, sump pits, manholes, sunken planters, sunken trenches for toilets/utilities |
| Counted as basement | **Underground tanks or plant rooms** |
| “Mean formation level” leases | Average of **highest + lowest** formation levels of the land on which any part stands, **same lowest floor** (incl. lowest basement) |
| Existing approved form | LandsD may recognise previously approved/no-objection height on the facts + legal advice |
| Rooftop features | If height is **not** an absolute cap, follow **JPN 5** size/height for rooftop ancillary / architectural features / signboards (if lease-permitted) |
| No explicit height SC | Pure aesthetic height **not** normally a DDH refusal |

**SD takeaway:** On old leases with no PR/GFA, height is **lowest basement/stilts to main-roof slab** — hide tanks in the basement storey count.

### 5. Stilts, fill, parking, plates, walls, projections (para. 7(c)–(j))

| Topic | Lock |
|---|---|
| **Stilts** | Discouraged even without anti-stilt SC. If no alternative: **minimise**; **guide max 8 m**; **not automatic**; architectural + landscape screening (App.); justifications + treatment with GBP |
| **Soil fill** under houses/gardens/flats on **flat** land | Generally **not** acceptable. Sloping/undulating: **≤2 m** normally OK; deeper on merits |
| **Parking** | Extra spaces / L/UL / undesignated areas → LAO PN **4/2014**. **Stacked** parking with **excessive headroom** not acceptable (avoids extra spaces) |
| **Transfer plate** | Allowed under DDH if **BA** accepts it as non-accountable GFA under BO (JPN 5 streamlining) |
| **Walls** | Follow **BA** on boundary / party / parapet / dividing walls |
| **External finish / shape** | LandsD would **not normally** restrict; existing-lease enforcement of this **normally waived** |
| **Encroachment** | Still LAO PN **4/2008** — no Government-land encroachment regardless of DDH |
| Canopies / architectural features over streets | Generally OK if no planning/aesthetic/env/road-safety objection; **BA usually followed** |
| Pipes/gutters over streets | Generally OK if projection **≤30 mm** at **≥2.5 m** above ground (**B(P)R 7(2)**) and no TD/HyD objection |
| Noise barriers over streets | If lease has NIA / noise-barrier SCs; design agreed by EPD, PlanD, HyD, TD, BD and matches approved NIA |
| Cladding/CW beyond lot | Entire IB conversion: PN **1/2010B**. Partial conversion/other buildings: normally if BA + TD/HyD etc.; **modification** + fee/premium |

**SD takeaway:** Cap stilts at **well under 8 m with screening**, keep garden fill **≤2 m**, and do **not** buy extra parking with tall stacks.

### 6. Process (paras. 6, 8–10)

- DDH/DD approval **bundled with GBP Stage 1** (PN 4/2018); in-principle approval is **not** proof of other lease compliance (area still Stages 2–3).
- List of aspects is **not exhaustive**; Director may add case-specific items.
- DLO **refusal** under DDH/DD after para. 7 (and extra aspects) needs **Building Committee III** (DD LandsD chair).
- Appeal to the Director; decision **final**.

**SD takeaway:** Fix DDH issues in the **Stage 1** GBP pack — there is no later separate DDH meeting unless BCIII on a refusal.

### Source

`PN 3_2020.pdf` | JPN 5 | LAO PN 4/2018, 4/2014, 4/2008, 1/2010B | **PN 3/2020A** (SC update). Text from OCR of a scanned PDF.
*Source Critical Summary:* `source_md/LAO Practice Note No. 3-2020 (27 April 2020)_CS.md`

---

<a id="pn-3-2020a"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 3/2020A — DDH clause (site coverage) and underground structures
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
August 2021 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Varies para 7(a)(i) of LAO PN No. 3/2020 | Aligns with **JPN No. 7** from **23 August 2021**

> Scope note: Streamlines **site coverage (SC)** (and de-facto **roofed-over area (ROA)**) accountability of **underground** structures under the lease DDH/SC clause. It is **not** a BO GFA code and **not** a grant of extra intensity. **Does not apply** to Cap. 121-exempted NT buildings.

---

## Regulatory Overview

JPN 7’s streamlined SC arrangements among BD, LandsD and PlanD take effect from 23 August 2021. This PN varies PN 3/2020 para 7(a)(i) only insofar as underground structures in SC calculation are concerned; other lease parameters stay unchanged.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. Leases with max GFA and/or plot ratio **plus** SC (paras 2–3)

| Rule | Lock |
|---|---|
| Objective | Flexibility in **underground space** within the lot — **not** an increase in intensity beyond existing lease |
| LandsD SC accountability | Generally adopt **BD’s** treatment of facilities/features for SC, **unless** explicit lease conditions prohibit |

**SD takeaway:** On GFA/PR + SC leases, design underground plant/parking to BD SC practice unless the lease text itself blocks that treatment.

---

### 2. Leases with SC (and height) but **no** max GFA **nor** max PR (para 4)

| Rule | Lock |
|---|---|
| Default | SC + height have been the intensity control; underground buildings/structures **not** already exempted from SC |
| Flexibility | Director of Lands **may**, unless the statutory town plan says otherwise, **disregard underground structures** when checking SC for development, redevelopment or A&A |
| Condition | Owner must show to the Director’s satisfaction that intensity would **not exceed** what is achievable under **existing** lease conditions |
| If flexibility would **increase** intensity | **Lease modification** is required |
| “Underground structures” | Director’s decision is **final and binding** |
| ROA leases (fn 1) | Same approach as SC |

**SD takeaway:** On SC/height-only leases, underground bulk is not a free SC gift — prove no extra intensity, or budget a modification.

---

### 3. Carve-outs and companions (paras 5–6)

| Item | Lock |
|---|---|
| Cap. 121 NTEH | PN **not applicable** |
| Flexibility into the SC clause | See **LAO PN No. 1/2004** |

**SD takeaway:** Do not apply this underground-SC stream to Cap. 121 houses; for rewriting the SC clause itself use PN 1/2004.

---

### Source

- `PN 3_2020A.pdf`
- Read with: **JPN No. 7**; **LAO PN No. 3/2020** para 7(a)(i); **LAO PN No. 1/2004**
*Source Critical Summary:* `source_md/LAO Practice Note No. 3-2020A (August 2021)_CS.md`

<a id="pn-5-2020"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 5/2020 — Professional Fees on Compensation Claims
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
30 June 2020 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Supersedes LAO PN 1/96 | Later varied by **PN 5/2020B** (and 5/2020A, superseded by 5/2020B)

> Scope note: **Admissible professional costs** when a claimant uses **more than one professional** on a land **acquisition/resumption** compensation claim. It is **not** a design or GBP PN. After Cap. 619 (14 Dec 2015), **HKIS scale charges are not used**.

## Regulatory Overview

Ordinances on acquisition/resumption often allow recovery of costs reasonably incurred employing professionals. Where several professionals are needed, fees are assessed on **time, manpower and resources** actually engaged; LandsD tests **reasonableness**. A **coordinator** among the professionals may be paid **in addition** if a genuine need is first established.

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. Fee basis (paras. 1–4)

| Rule | Detail |
|---|---|
| Multi-professional | Admissible if reasonably required for assessment/submission of the claim |
| Quantum | Calculated from each professional’s claim vs **time / manpower / other resources**; department tests reasonableness |
| HKIS scales | **Not applicable** after Competition Ordinance **14.12.2015** |
| Coordinator | Optional; extra fee only if genuine need established first |

**SD takeaway:** On resumption, budget **time-based** multi-consultant fees — not HKIS scale percentages — and justify a coordinator separately.

### Source

`PN 5_2020.pdf` | **PN 5/2020B** (operative supplement). Text from OCR of a scanned PDF.
*Source Critical Summary:* `source_md/LAO Practice Note No. 5-2020 (30 June 2020)_CS.md`

---

<a id="pn-5-2020b"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 5/2020B — Payment of Professional Fees in Compensation Claims
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
15 December 2025 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Immediate effect | Supplements **LAO PN 5/2020**; **supersedes** **LAO PN 5/2020A**

> Scope note: **Claim-form and guideline update** for reimbursement of professional fees reasonably incurred in **statutory compensation** claims. It is **not** a lease GFA/SC/BH instrument and does not set design parameters.

---

## Regulatory Overview

This PN issues a new **Application Form for Claim of Professional Fees** (Appendix I) and **guidelines** (Appendix II) for claimants and the firms acting for them. It does not change how compensation itself is assessed; it only structures the fee-reimbursement claim.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. What the claim must cover (App I)

| Item | Lock |
|---|---|
| Submission | Mail or in person to the **DLO or LandsD section**; Government not bound to accept |
| Settlement | Claim is **full and final** for professional costs under the stated Ordinance(s) for that land-interest claim — **no further fee claim** |
| Claim types (tick) | Resumption; easement & other permanent rights; temporary occupation; business disturbance; severance (marine access or resumption); removal of graves/urns; other |
| Related projects | Disclose other compensation claims in the **same and/or related project(s)** of one development (e.g. Cap. **124** resumption plus Cap. **370** roads and/or Cap. **127** reclamation for one public-housing scheme) |
| Mandatory attachments (#) | Claimant **authorisation**; disbursement breakdown **with receipts**; valuation report(s) if not already provided; **separate Claim Form (Annex) per professional and technician/paralegal** |
| Without # docs | LandsD **may not** process, validate, or quantify the claim |

**SD takeaway:** If the office is claiming fees on a resumption/roads/reclamation package, file **one consolidated** professional-fee claim and flag **related projects** — missing authorisation, receipts, or per-person timesheets can stall payment.

---

### 2. Time recording and reasonableness (Annex + App II)

| Rule | Lock |
|---|---|
| Non-solicitor professionals | Work codes 1–8: inspection; research/analysis; assessment reports; team coordination; paper/letters; Government meetings; claimant/team meetings; others (e.g. Lands Tribunal) |
| Solicitors | Codes 1–6: rights/procedure advice; Government letters; correspondence (other than title requisitions); assessment/monitoring; meetings; **title requisitions** and document perusal |
| Proof of time | Meetings need minutes/notes/summaries/correspondence with attendees, **start/end time**, location, agenda; endorse by signature or email where practicable |
| Multi-disciplinary team | **One consolidated** fee claim for all professionals; optional **team coordinator** (mark “(Coordinator)”) |
| Reasonableness | LandsD weighs nature, type, extent, complexity, and **qualifications / post-qualification experience** |

**SD takeaway:** Keep dated, attendee-endorsed meeting records if an AP/RLA/surveyor is on the compensation team — LandsD will not take hours on trust.

---

### Source

- `PN 5_2020B.pdf`
- Read with: **LAO PN 5/2020** (parent); applicable compensation ordinances named in the claim (Caps **124**, **370**, **127** as examples)
*Source Critical Summary:* `source_md/LAO Practice Note No. 5-2020B (December 2025)_CS.md`

<a id="pn-2-2021"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 2/2021 — Building Covenant Extensions
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
3 June 2021 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Supersedes LAO PNs 4/2020, 5/2008, 5/1996 (APSS 4/96), 5/1994 (APSS 2/94)

> Scope note: How to **extend the building covenant (BC)** period on a new or modified lease. Completing the BC means finishing the development **and obtaining OP** within the BC period. **Does not apply** to indigenous-villager small houses under Cap. 121. It is **not** a BO occupation-permit PN.

## Regulatory Overview

Apply to the relevant DLO/section when OP will miss the BC date. LandsD generally processes extensions **closer to expiry** (early extension possible to support LACO pre-sale consent). Extensions are in **multiples of 3 months**, **premium** on a rising % of land value by year of extension. This PN adds a **1–3 year** streamlined multi-year extension and a **3-month deposit** path when OP is imminent.

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. Premium scale (para. 2)

| Extension year from original BC expiry | % of land value |
|---|---|
| 1st | **2%** |
| 2nd | **4%** |
| 3rd | **8%** |
| 4th | **14%** |
| 5th | **22%** |
| 6th and after | **32%** |

A few exceptions exist. Any approval is on LandsD terms including premium.

**SD takeaway:** Programme OP inside the original BC — each extra year **doubles-plus** the land-value levy.

### 2. Streamlined extension **>1 year and ≤3 years** (paras. 3–5)

With effect from this PN, LandsD will consider **one** application for **>1 year up to 3 years** (still in **3-month multiples**) instead of successive 1-year extensions. Lodge:

- AP certificate: **superstructure building contract awarded** (incl. commencement info);
- AP certificate: date OP is **reasonably expected**;
- copy of BA **consent to commence superstructure**.

If, at the end of that extended period, reasonable effort is **not** shown, LandsD may grant **shorter** further extensions, **refuse**, or take **lease enforcement**.

**SD takeaway:** A 3-year BC extension needs a **let superstructure contract + BA commencement consent + OP date** on the AP certificate — not a paper programme.

### 3. Three-month “OP imminent” extension (para. 6)

If the job is advanced and OP is expected **just after** BC (or extended BC) expiry: apply for **3 months** with AP certificate of **reasonable expectation of OP within those 3 months**. Premium is paid as a **deposit**; if OP issues **on or before** the 3-month end, the premium is **refunded without interest**.

**SD takeaway:** Use the 3-month deposit route only when OP is genuinely in the window — otherwise the deposit is kept as premium.

### 4. Other

- “Commence to operate” clauses may exist **in addition to or instead of** BC.
- Sample letters: Apps. II(a), II(b), III.
- Each case at LandsD’s **absolute discretion** as landlord.

**SD takeaway:** BC / commence-to-operate dates belong on the SD programme next to OP.

### Source

`PN 2_2021.pdf` | LACO pre-sale consent timing | superseded PNs listed above. Text from OCR of a scanned PDF.
*Source Critical Summary:* `source_md/LAO Practice Note No. 2-2021 (3 June 2021)_CS.md`

<a id="pn-4-2021"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 4/2021 — Approval or Consent under Lease
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
31 December 2021 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department

> Scope note: Classifies when LandsD **premium** is / is not charged for lease **approval or consent**, and which items can be granted at **GBP** checking. It is **not** a BO plan code and **does not apply** to Cap. 121–exempted buildings. Does not fetter the Director’s lease discretion.

---

## Regulatory Overview

A Government lease (including Conditions of Sale / Grant / Exchange) may require the lessor’s prior approval or consent; the Director may attach terms including premium. Premium is generally **not** charged where the consent does not relax lease restrictions, or (Part II of Appendix A) where it does relax them but the proposal is acceptable to the concerned bureau(x)/department(s); typical premium-chargeable cases are listed in Appendix B.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. When premium is / is not charged (paras 1–4)

| Situation | Premium |
|---|---|
| Consent **pursuant to** lease clauses, **no** relaxation of requirements/restrictions | **No** premium — Appendix A **Part (I)** |
| Consent that **relaxes** lease requirements/restrictions | Director’s **absolute discretion**; premium **may** be charged if consent would **enhance development potential or land value** |
| Listed relaxations in Appendix A **Part (II)** | Premium **may not** be charged **if** the proposal is acceptable to the concerned B/D(s) |
| Typical premium cases | Appendix **B** (general reference) |

**SD takeaway:** Map every SD departure from lease (VAP, NBA/setback, parking numbers, MLP, GFA exclusions) to Appendix A vs B **before** locking the GBP — that is the premium path.

---

### 2. GBP-stage grant and 16-week premium track (paras 5–6; fn 2; PN 4/2018)

| Track | What | Timing / output |
|---|---|---|
| Streamlined (no separate application) | Appendix A **Part (I) specified clauses** + **all** Part (II) clauses | Generally assessed/granted at **GBP checking** |
| Fast-track **premium** | Specified Appendix **B** situations (marked **#**) | LandsD aims to advise premium within **16 weeks** of a **valid** application |
| After premium + admin fee paid | Consent letter **with** GBP approval | Permits development per the agreed GBP for the **lifetime of the building(s)** |
| Owner option | Apply for **lease modification** instead of lifetime approval/consent | Established modification mechanism |
| Lifetime of consent | Approval/consent under specific lease provision(s) | Effective for **lifetime of the building(s)** for which the GBP was approved (fn 2) |

**SD takeaway:** Put Part (I)/(II) items on the first GBP so consent rides the plan check; budget **16 weeks** + admin fee if any Appendix B **#** item is in the scheme.

---

### 3. Appendix A Part (I) — no-premium, no-relaxation consents

Typical clauses (no relaxation of lease requirements):

| Item | Clause type |
|---|---|
| 1 | Design and disposition (or design, disposition **and height**) |
| 2 | Recreational facilities |
| 3 | Office accommodation for watchmen and caretakers |
| 4 | Owners’ corporation / owners’ committee office |
| 5 | Quarters for watchmen and caretakers |
| 6 | Diversion/relocation of existing water mains, sewers, pipeworks, drainage, footpath, etc. **along the diverted route delineated in the lease** |
| 7 | Formation of green / yellow / brown / other **coloured areas** (areas **outside** lot, usually Government land on the lease plan, to be maintained or handed back) — EOT for coloured-area formation is Appendix B Item 8 |
| 8 | Deposit of car park layout plan |
| 9 | Landscaping |
| 10 | Preservation of trees (**except** retrospective consent for unauthorised felling/interference) |
| 11 | Natural terrain |
| 12 | Impact assessments (e.g. NIA) |
| 13 | Offensive trades (see LAO PN **3/2021**) |
| 14 | Provision of sales office and show flats |
| 15 | Restriction on alienation before compliance (**Presale Consent** under LandsD Consent Scheme) |
| 16 | DMC incorporating Management Agreement (if any) |
| 17 | **MLP** approval (including later amendments) submitted **after** lease execution to **comply with** lease — **not** if the amendment varies lease parameters or is an Appendix B Item 5 “control” MLP |

These Part (I) specified clauses **might generally** be assessed/granted at GBP checking.

**SD takeaway:** Recreational GFA, watchmen rooms, OC office, tree/landscape, car-park layout, and post-grant MLP-for-compliance are normally **GBP-stage, nil-premium** — do not treat them as modifications.

---

### 4. Appendix A Part (II) — relaxation, generally nil premium if B/D accepts (GBP-stage)

| Item | Relaxation | Preconditions in this PN |
|---|---|---|
| 1 Vehicular access | Vary or **widen** VAP along the **same street** | Ground level of the lot **not** permitted for commercial purposes; **does not include addition** of a VAP |
| 2 Noise barriers | Barriers within the lot **projecting beyond lot boundary over Government land** | “Noise Barrier” clause **plus** “NIA” clause in **residential** Conditions of Sale |
| 3 Building separation | Vary **projected façade length** from CoS prescription | **BD** satisfied with the proposal; **JPN 6** for SBD building-separation compliance |
| 4 Disabled persons’ parking | Vary **number and dimension** as BA may require and approve | Generally not premium-chargeable; inclusion of the clause in **old leases** may be by **technical modification** |
| 5 NBA / DRA / WRA / building setback | Listed structures **inside** those areas | Technically acceptable to the proponent department(s); **JPN 6** for SBD setback; noise barriers as required under lease or to implement approved NIA are included under fence/boundary wall |

**At-grade features allowed in NBA/DRA/WRA/setback (Item 5):** planter/curb; open driveway/ramp/road curb/EVA; open pavement/footpath/walkway/steps/stairs; open swimming pool/pool deck/jacuzzi; water feature; smoke vent / floor-type smoke outlet / basement air intake; surface channel / drainage outlets / draw pits.

**Common-use features:** fence/boundary wall (incl. lot-boundary utility cabinets); usual appurtenances (water/electricity/gas meter cabinets); garden fence/planter wall; entrance/metal/drop/crash gates; covered walkway at GF or podium / trellis; outdoor A/C plant; canopy covering **common** areas.

**Safety / other:** FS installations (street hydrant, sprinkler inlet, FS inlet, etc.); parapet of ramping driveways; vertical green features; minor structures holding pipes/drains; doors **outside** NBA/setback but **swinging over** it; temporary/supporting structures during construction.

All Part (II) consents **might generally** be assessed/granted at GBP checking.

**SD takeaway:** You can usually put planters, open EVA/driveways, FS inlets, meter cabinets, and swinging doors in NBA/DRA/WRA/setback **without premium** if the department accepts — **adding** a VAP, commercial-ground VAP moves, or extra parking numbers are **not** this track.

---

### 5. Appendix B — typical premium-chargeable consents

| # | Item | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| **#1** | Relax setback-area restriction on the **existing lease plan** under the Building Setback Clause where the **Director** grants consent | **JPN 6** for SBD setback; Item 5 Part (II) structures in NBA/DRA/WRA/setback are **not** this premium item |
| **#2** | Exclude **aboveground ancillary parking** accepted by BD on technical grounds from GFA | — |
| **#3** | Vary **number and dimension** of parking or L/UL required under lease | Contrast disabled-parking variation in Part (II) Item 4 |
| **#4** | Vary VAP **other than** Part (II) Item 1 (same-street widen/vary, no commercial GF) | Includes **adding** a VAP |
| 5 | Amend approved **MLP or control drawings** that the lease **ties** the development to | Contrast Part (I) Item 17 compliance MLP |
| 6 | Cut away on **Government land** under Set Back Clause for **exceptional geotechnical** reasons | — |
| 7 | Divert existing pipework/drainage **or** vary alignment/layout/dimension of NBA, DRA, WRA | Contrast Part (II) Item 5 **structures** (nil premium) |
| 8 | **EOT** for formation of coloured areas if the expiry date **differs** from the Building Covenant | — |

**#** items: generally GBP-stage; LandsD aims to advise premium within **16 weeks** of a valid application.

**SD takeaway:** Aboveground car-park GFA exclusion, extra/fewer parking stalls, extra VAPs, and lease-tied MLP/control-drawing changes are **premium** — lock counts and access points against the lease before GBP.

---

### Source

- `PN 4_2021.pdf`
- Read with: **LAO PN 4/2018** (GBP checking stages); **JPN 6** (building separation / setback); **LAO PN 3/2021** (offensive trades)

<a id="pn-1-2022"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 1/2022 — KTN/FLN Remaining Phase land exchange
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
16 February 2022 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Supersedes LAO PN Nos. **1/2014, 1/2014A and 1/2014D** (1/2014B and 1/2014C already superseded by 1/2014D)

> Scope note: In-situ lease modification / land exchange for **Remaining Phase** private-development sites on the Kwu Tung North and Fanling North ODPs (hatched on LandsD reference plans). It is **not** an OZP. Deadlines in para 13 were later varied by **PN 1/2022A**.

---

## Regulatory Overview

Owners of private lots inside Remaining Phase hatched sites may apply for exchange for users permitted under OZPs **S/KTN/2** and **S/FLN/2** (or later amendments) or by TPB. Phase 1 application/BBTO deadlines (30 November 2014 / 30 September 2017) are historical. Lodge at CES/NDA, Landmark North, Sheung Shui. Accepted applications are published (plan of private land) and occupants notified.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. General criteria — Appendix III

| Criterion | Lock |
|---|---|
| Location | Sites planned for **private** development on adopted KTN and FLN **ODPs** (and later ODP amendments) |
| Size / ownership | Proposed surrender **≥ 4,000 m²**; all private lots under **single owner or JV** |
| Configuration | Reasonably regular; **contiguous**; **no intervening** lots not owned by applicant; fragmented lots not accepted |
| Confinement | Only lots inside a hatched planned private site; lots in road / GIC / OS / GB / public or subsidised housing **not normally** accepted |
| Access | Proper **vehicular access** feasible |
| OZP | Use and parameters comply with KTN and FLN OZPs |
| Residual of the ODP site | If not assembling **all** private land in the ODP development site: **layout plan** proving decent full-site utilisation **without loss of ODP-planned potential** (site boundaries may shift after NDA detailed design) |

**SD takeaway:** Assemble ≥ 4,000 m² inside one hatched ODP private site, keep the block contiguous, and if you leave residual private lots, prove the rest of the ODP site still yields its planned GFA.

---

### 2. Occupants — 4 July 2013 baseline (paras 8–10, App IV)

| Item | Lock |
|---|---|
| Eligible occupants | Individuals occupying surrender lots **as at 4 July 2013 or thereafter** as registered in LandsD’s **pre-clearance survey** |
| Applicant’s duty | Show they accepted **compensation or rehousing comparable** to Government NDA clearance (May 2018 non-means-tested option included). Applicant-paid package is **not premium-deductible** |
| Early Departure Scheme | Occupants may take Government early compensation/rehousing; then para 8 duty **drops** for that occupant but applicant **reimburses** Government (compensation + cash value of rehousing + admin cost) |
| Timing vs BBTO | After **PBTO**, before **BBTO**: either signed acceptances, **or** per-occupant **Undertaking** (keep offers open **3 years** from execution; reimburse if they later take Government EDS) **plus Deposit** equal to that reimbursement sum; plus evidence of **vacant possession** on a reasonable programme |
| Release of Undertaking/Deposit | Occupant accepted applicant’s offer; **or** reimbursement paid; **or** **3 years** from execution |
| Disputes | LandsD may **suspend** processing |

**SD takeaway:** Occupant clearance is on the critical path to BBTO — survey everyone on the 4 July 2013 register, price comparable rehousing, and allow 3-year EDS tail after execution.

---

### 3. Processing requirements — Appendix IV

| Item | Lock |
|---|---|
| Title / VP | Unified titles; vacant possession by a specified deadline; **free of encumbrances** before execution |
| Time | Conclude **before** Government resumption; specified period unless Government extends |
| Government land | Re-grant **may** include adjoining/intervening GL **incapable** of reasonable separate alienation; GL that **can** be separately alienated is **not** normally thrown in to match the ODP blob |

**SD takeaway:** Do not assume the re-grant fills out the ODP site with GL — only leftover GL that cannot stand alone.

---

### 4. Submission pack — Appendix I (design-facing)

Must: 2 copies of land grants (LR/solicitor certified ≤ 1 month); 1:1000 plan; 2 copies historical+current ownership; company docs / mortgagee letters / solicitor share list if multi-owned; TPB letter if any; agent authorisation; property identity.

Facilitate: OZP extract; assignment/Deed Poll plans; **2 sets of sketch plans and development parameters**; occupant schedules (current **and** 4 July 2013-or-after who left); development programme.

**SD takeaway:** First pack needs parameter sketches plus the 4 July 2013 occupant schedules — not a late add-on.

---

### 5. Premium, vacant possession, original deadlines (paras 11–13, fn)

| Item | Lock |
|---|---|
| After PBTO | Exchange **not processed further** until para. 8–10 occupant requirements are met |
| If approved | Terms including **premium** as LandsD demands |
| Vacant possession | Actual VP of private land **no later than acceptance of BBTO (with premium)** |
| Original clocks (this PN) | Apply by **15 August 2022**. Processing **stops** if BBTO with premium is not issued **and accepted** by **30 June 2023** (tied to Government resumption). Admin fee **not refunded**. Late applications **not considered** |
| Later variation | **PN 1/2022A** moves the BBTO (with premium) stop to **31 December 2023** |
| Standard-rate option | Government to announce NDA standard-rate premium **before end of March 2022**. IB Pilot **PN 1/2021** **does not** apply to these exchanges |

**SD takeaway:** VP must be ready at **BBTO acceptance**; treat **15 Aug 2022 / 30 Jun 2023** as the original clocks and **1/2022A** as the later BBTO extension — do not use the IB 1/2021 standard-rate pilot on these lots.

---

### Source

- `PN 1_2022.pdf` (page 5 recovered by OCR: paras 11–16, 16 February 2022)
- Varied by: **LAO PN No. 1/2022A** (BBTO to 31 December 2023). Read with OZPs **S/KTN/2**, **S/FLN/2**; **LAO PN No. 3/2022** (standard rates)

## LAO Practice Note No. 1/2022A — KTN/FLN Remaining Phase land exchange deadline
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
June 2023 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Immediate effect | Varies and supplements LAO PN No. 1/2022

> Scope note: Moves the **binding basic terms offer (with premium)** cut-off for accepted Kwu Tung North / Fanling North Remaining Phase land-exchange applications. It is **not** a new application window and does **not** change OZP/ODP design parameters in PN 1/2022.

---

## Regulatory Overview

This PN varies only paragraph 13 of LAO PN No. 1/2022. All other provisions of PN 1/2022 remain valid.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. Processing cut-off (para 2)

| Item | Lock |
|---|---|
| Varied para 13 wording | Any **accepted** application **ceases to be processed** if a binding basic terms offer (with premium) cannot be **issued and accepted on or before 31 December 2023** |
| Unchanged | Remainder of PN 1/2022 |

<a id="pn-2-2022"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 2/2022 — COVID-19 building-covenant extension at nil premium
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
March 2022 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Measure announced **14 March 2022**

> Scope note: One-off **up to six calendar months** BC (and CtO / coloured-area / GA completion) extension at **nil premium**. It is **not** a general BC-premium code — further time still follows LAO PN No. 2/2021. LandsD applies it **without application**.

---

## Regulatory Overview

The Measure covers all development types with **unfulfilled BC as at 14 March 2022**, including Cap. 121 works and industrial-building conversion under special waiver. It does **not** apply to leases executed after 14 March 2022, to BCs not imposed by LandsD (e.g. Cap. 545 Lands Tribunal orders), or to cases already BC-fulfilled before **1 January 2022** with only formality outstanding.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. Who gets six months and from when (paras 2–3)

| Case | Nil-premium extension |
|---|---|
| BC (or already-extended BC) **not yet expired** on 14 March 2022 | LandsD offers **six calendar months from that expiry**; offer letter issued within **3 calendar months before** expiry |
| BC already **expired** on 14 March 2022 | Up to six calendar months commencing on the **expiry date or 1 January 2022, whichever later** |
| Premium already paid on earlier extensions | **Not refunded** |
| Application from owner | **Not required** |

Fn 1–2: “BC period” includes **commence-to-operate (CtO)** and associated formation/construction periods (coloured areas, Government Accommodation) even if those dates differ from BC.

**SD takeaway:** Do not programme a 14 March 2022-era unfulfilled BC as immediately in breach — LandsD will add up to six months at nil premium; still freeze GA/coloured-area completion dates against the same Measure.

---

### 2. Time beyond six months (para 4 and fn 6–7)

| Rule | Lock |
|---|---|
| Further extension | May attract premium under established practice |
| Sliding scale (PN 2/2021 para 3) | The Measure’s up-to-six-month nil period is **disregarded** when counting the year of extension |
| Illustration (fn 6) | Original BC 31 March 2022, 1-year extension sought: **1 Apr–30 Sep 2022** nil; **1 Oct 2022–31 Mar 2023** charged at **2% p.a.** of land value |
| Further 6 months to 30 Sep 2023 (fn 7) | Still assessed as **1st-year** 2% p.a. because the nil six months are ignored |

**SD takeaway:** A programme that needs more than the Measure’s six months still pays PN 2/2021 premium, but the nil six months do **not** push you into a higher sliding-scale year.

---

### Source

- `PN 2_2022.pdf`
- Read with: **LAO PN No. 2/2021** (BC-extension premium sliding scale)
*Source Critical Summary:* `source_md/LAO Practice Note No. 2-2022 (March 2022)_CS.md`

<a id="pn-3-2022"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 3/2022 — Standard-rate premium for KTN/FLN Remaining Phase exchanges
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
March 2022 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Option for applications under **LAO PN No. 1/2022**

> Scope note: Optional **standard-rate** land premium (instead of case-by-case) for lease modification / land exchange in the **Remaining Phase** of Kwu Tung North and Fanling North NDAs. It is **not** an intensity code. Rates and BBTO date were later varied by **PN 3/2022A**.

---

## Regulatory Overview

The Option must be exercised **not later than PBTO acceptance** via the Appendix I Option Form and is **irreversible** thereafter (no premium appeal; fees forfeited if the applicant walks; re-application after the PN 1/2022 application deadline of **15 August 2022** will not be considered). This extract’s rates are valid through the then PN 1/2022 processing period to **30 June 2023**.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. Standard rates in this PN (para 3) — later superseded by PN 3/2022A

Per **GFA** except agricultural use (**per site area**).

| NDA | Ag. use | Before: non-res. building^ | Before: residential building | After: non-res.# | After: residential |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Kwu Tung North** | $4,000/m² | $20,000/m² | $45,000/m² | $35,000/m² | $60,000/m² |
| **Fanling North** | $4,000/m² | $20,000/m² | $45,000/m² | $30,000/m² | $55,000/m² |

| Note | Lock |
|---|---|
| ^ | Includes industrial; LandsD type decision final |
| # | Commercial and modern industrial; **excludes** columbarium, PFS, DG storage, offensive trades; **hotel / hotel ancillary = commercial**; LandsD type decision final |

**SD takeaway:** Do not use this table for live premium — **PN 3/2022A** replaced these figures and moved the validity date to 31 December 2023.

---

### 2. Premium formula (para 4) — **premium = A × B − C × D − E** (not negotiable; negative → nil)

| Factor | Meaning |
|---|---|
| **A** | Maximum **total GFA after** exchange as stipulated in the exchange document(s), determined by LandsD |
| **B** | After-exchange rate(s) by NDA. **Residential rate** on all GFA **not prohibiting private residential**; remainder at non-residential rate |
| **C** | Site area of **Agricultural land** surrendered and/or **permissible GFA** of **Building land** surrendered |
| **D** | Before-exchange rate(s). On Building land: residential-building rate on GFA not prohibiting residential; remainder at non-res. building rate |
| **E** | Deductible **construction cost of Government Accommodation** required to be built and assigned/handed back or OMM’d for public use — only if the proponent department conveys the figure **before** the Option deadline; else **E = 0**. GA eligibility at LandsD discretion |

| GFA rules for A (fn 3) | Lock |
|---|---|
| GA floor space | **May be disregarded** in max total GFA |
| NOFA stated for GA | Convert **NOFA : GFA = 1 : 1.8** (e.g. 100 m² NOFA = 180 m² GFA) |
| Other floor space | Disregarded **only** if **expressly GFA-exempted** in the exchange document |
| No max total GFA in the document | **This PN does not apply** |
| Pre-1987 IBs (fn 1) | May opt **only** these NDA rates, **not** PN 1/2021 IB standard rates |

**SD takeaway:** Freeze **lease GFA (A)**, residential vs non-residential split (drives B), surrender Ag vs Building land (C/D), and whether GA is in the deal (E and 1:1.8 NOFA) **before** ticking the Option — there is no later appeal.

---

### 3. Agricultural vs Building land for C (fn 4–5)

| Class | How assessed |
|---|---|
| **Agricultural land** | Old schedule / new grant ag lots, and ag portion of mixed lots — **site area** × ag rate |
| **Building land** | Land where building is allowed, and building portion of mixed lots. Old schedule descriptive terms that count as Building land include house, building, mat shed, boat hut, temple, nunnery, latrine, shop, cow shed, cattle shed, duck shed, cow byre, pigsty, school, or other uses LandsD may determine — treated as **residential building use** (residential not prohibited) |
| Permissible GFA missing | Adopt **plot ratio 1.33** |
| Untraceable lease | Use and site area at LandsD discretion |

Applicant should submit a Cap. 473 **Authorised Land Surveyor** Survey Submission (Record Plan, Land Boundary Plan, Survey Report) to support C (para 5; page 4 of this extract is blank).

**SD takeaway:** Survey and classify every surrender lot as Ag (site area) or Building (GFA or PR 1.33) at SD — that split is the credit side of premium.

---

### Source

- `PN 3_2022.pdf`
- Varied by: **LAO PN No. 3/2022A**. Read with: **LAO PN No. 1/2022**; **PN 1/2021** (pre-1987 IB rates excluded here)

<a id="pn-3-2022a"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 3/2022A — KTN/FLN standard-rate premium (revised rates and deadline)
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
June 2023 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Immediate effect | Varies and supplements LAO PN No. 3/2022

> Scope note: Re-sets **standard-rate land premium** figures and the **Option** deadline for Remaining Phase Kwu Tung North / Fanling North land exchanges, tracking PN 1/2022A’s BBTO date. It is **not** a new exchange window.

---

## Regulatory Overview

Paragraphs 2 and 3 of PN 3/2022 are replaced to match the PN 1/2022A processing deadline of **31 December 2023**. Applicants who accepted a PBTO before this PN without submitting the Option Form get a one-off opt-in until **28 July 2023**.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. Option mechanics (varied para 2)

| Item | Lock |
|---|---|
| BBTO (with premium) | Must be **accepted on or before 31 December 2023** or the application ceases |
| When to opt | Not later than acceptance of **PBTO**, via Appendix I Option Form |
| After PBTO accepted | Option is **irreversible**; **no premium appeal** under BBTO |
| Walking away | Application fees **forfeited**; **re-application will not be considered** |

**SD takeaway:** Standard-rate election must be on the table **before PBTO acceptance** — after that the GFA/use mix used in A×B−C×D−E is locked with no appeal.

---

### 2. Standard rates valid to 31 December 2023 (varied para 3)

All rates **per GFA** except agricultural use (**per site area**).

| NDA | Ag. use | Before: non-res. building^ | Before: residential building | After: non-res.# | After: residential |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Kwu Tung North** | $4,000/m² | $15,000/m² | $30,000/m² | $27,500/m² | $40,000/m² |
| **Fanling North** | $4,000/m² | $15,000/m² | $30,000/m² | $22,500/m² | $37,500/m² |

| Note | Lock |
|---|---|
| ^ Non-res. building use before exchange | Includes **industrial**; LandsD type decision is final |
| # Non-res. use after exchange | Includes **commercial** and **modern industrial**; **excludes** columbarium, PFS, dangerous-goods storage, offensive trades; **hotel and hotel ancillary = commercial**; LandsD type decision is final |

**SD takeaway:** Price the scheme off **post-exchange GFA by use** (residential rate on all GFA not prohibiting private residential) using these KTN/FLN figures — they replace PN 3/2022’s higher table.

---

### 3. Late Option for pre-PN PBTOs (para 4)

| Situation | Lock |
|---|---|
| PBTO accepted **before** this PN, **no** Option Form yet | May opt at these revised rates by submitting the Option Form **on or before 28 July 2023** |
| Form not in by that date | Deemed to have chosen **case-by-case** premium |

**SD takeaway:** If PBTO is already accepted without an Option Form, 28 July 2023 is the last day to switch to standard rates.

---

### Source

- `PN 3_2022A.pdf`
- Read with: **LAO PN No. 3/2022** (formula A×B−C×D−E); **LAO PN Nos. 1/2022 and 1/2022A**

<a id="pn-4-2022"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 4/2022 — Installation of Solar Photovoltaic Systems in Private Developments
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
11 May 2022 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department

> Scope note: Lease facilitation for **ancillary** solar PV on private lots. It is **not** a BO structural code, **not** for dedicated commercial solar farms, and **not applicable** to Cap. 121–exempted buildings. NTEH roof/stairhood PV is pointed to a separate LandsD pamphlet.

---

## Regulatory Overview

LandsD will generally treat solar PV (panels, inverters, meters, boards, cables, supporting structures forming a complete grid-connected installation) as **ancillary** to the permitted lease user if the electricity is for the **sole use** of occupiers and of the building/accommodation **on the lot**, so a separate user application is generally **not** required. The installation must still comply with **all other** lease conditions; space **underneath** the PV **shall not be enclosed**.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. Ancillary-use test (para 2)

| Rule | Lock |
|---|---|
| User | Generally accepted as ancillary to permitted lease use |
| Electricity | **Sole use** by occupiers in the building/accommodation **within the lot**; **not significantly in excess** of what those occupiers/building normally require |
| Under-croft | Space underneath the solar PV systems **shall not in any event be enclosed** |
| Other lease conditions | Still must be complied with |
| Separate LandsD user application | Generally **not** required if the above holds |

**SD takeaway:** Keep PV as a **lot-serving** array with **open** undercroft — do not enclose the space or size the array as an export farm.

---

### 2. BO / MWCS vs full GBP path (paras 3–4)

| Path | When | LandsD plans |
|---|---|---|
| **MWCS** simplified works | Supporting structure meets BD **Technical Guidelines on MWCS** (among other matters, **not higher than 1.5 m** — MW items **1.50** and **3.50**) | Separate building plans/drawings to LandsD **generally not** required |
| Full BA approval + consent to commence | Structure **does not** meet the Technical Guidelines / not under MWCS | Engage an **AP**; BD refers plans to LandsD under lease via **Centralised Processing** (**PNAP ADM-2**) |

**SD takeaway:** Design PV supports **≤ 1.5 m** under MWCS to avoid a LandsD GBP round-trip; taller frames go through BA → LandsD CPS.

---

### 3. Exclusions (paras 5, 7)

| Out of this PN | What happens |
|---|---|
| Dedicated **commercial solar farms** on private lots (whether or not buildings/structures are prohibited under lease) | Considered **separately**, case by case |
| Buildings exempted from the BO by **Cap. 121** | PN **not applicable** |
| NTEH roof or stairhood (new and existing) | Use LandsD pamphlet **“Building New Territories Exempted Houses”** (landsd.gov.hk) |

**SD takeaway:** Do not rely on this PN for a commercial PV farm or a Cap. 121 house — those are different tracks.

---

### Source

- `PN 4_2022.pdf`
- Read with: BD **Technical Guidelines on MWCS**; **PNAP ADM-2**; LandsD NTEH pamphlet
*Source Critical Summary:* `source_md/LAO Practice Note No. 4-2022 (May 2022)_CS.md`

---

<a id="pn-5-2022"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 5/2022 — Self-Certification of Sales Office and Show Flats
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
May 2022 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Forms: Rev. May 2022

> Scope note: Streamlined **lease approval** for temporary **sales office, show flats and related marketing** in **private residential or composite C/R** developments under the “Provision of sales office and show flats” clause. It is **not** a BO occupation permit and does **not** waive other lease conditions. Guidance Notes live on landsd.gov.hk (`PN 5_2022 Guidance.pdf`).

---

## Regulatory Overview

Where the lease requires the Director’s written approval to erect separate temporary structures and/or use parts of buildings on the lot as sales office/show flats/marketing, approval is **normally** given if scale and period are **temporary and reasonable**. The lot owner may appoint a registered professional to submit a **self-certification of compliance** (Form 1 + Form 2) instead of a conventional consent application.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. Who, when, how long (paras 2–4; Form 1)

| Rule | Lock |
|---|---|
| Eligible RP | **AP** / **RPS (General Practice)** / **RPS (Building Surveying)** / **Registered Architect** |
| DLO | Includes **Railway Development Section** |
| When to submit SCC | **After** issuance of **pre-sale consent** under the LandsD Consent Scheme |
| Validity of approval | Until the owner lodges the **Certificate of Compliance** application |
| Premises | Location and **GFA** as shown on **GBP approved by LandsD**; Form 1 states GFA in m² |
| Period | From the stated start date **to the date of CC application** |
| Attachments | Certified **full land search** + lease; **Consent Scheme** consent letter; plan of Premises GFA/location on the LandsD-approved GBP |

**SD takeaway:** Park sales-office/show-flat GFA on the **LandsD-approved GBP** and do not file the SCC until **presale consent** is in hand — approval dies when the **CC** application is lodged.

---

### 2. Random check vs deemed approval (paras 5–7)

| Outcome | Clock | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| Valid SCC entered in register | Random full-check selection | RP told within **10 working days** of Receipt Date whether selected |
| **Not** selected | Letter of acknowledgement within **10 working days** | **Deemed approval** under the Clause, containing Form 2 para 2 terms |
| **Selected** | Result within **8 weeks** (check + **on-site inspection**) | If fail: fresh Form 1 + Form 2 after rectification; if pass: DLO **approval letter** (Director may add terms) |
| Deemed approval is not | Acknowledgement of **other** lease conditions | LandsD may **revoke/withdraw** if SCC is non-compliant or Form 2 is breached |

**SD takeaway:** Budget **10 WD** for a no-check deemed approval or **8 weeks** if drawn for full check — do not occupy/market on the SCC alone until the acknowledgement or approval letter is issued.

---

### 3. Owner’s locks on expiry (Form 2 para 2)

| Term | Design / lease lock |
|---|---|
| Statutory licences | Owner must obtain all licences/permissions; if not obtained, Approval is **deemed withdrawn** |
| End of Period / revocation | Premises **cease** sales/show use and revert to **all** GSC/SCs including **user and maximum GFA**; temporary structures **demolished** at owner’s cost |
| Other lease conditions | Remain in force; SCC/Approval does **not** prove other compliance |
| Re-entry | Lease re-entry proviso **extends** to Form 2 (a)–(e) breaches |
| Third parties | No Contracts (Rights of Third Parties) Ordinance rights; DMC/mortgagee rights **not** prejudiced |
| Revocation | LandsD may revoke **at any time** without compensation |

**SD takeaway:** Treat sales office/show flats as **temporary GFA** that must vanish (and any temporary structure come down) by **CC application** — they cannot be left as permanent common or clubhouse space without a fresh lease path.

---

### Source

- `PN 5_2022.pdf`
- Read with: LandsD **Guidance Notes on Provision of Sales Office and Show Flats**; Consent Scheme / CC practice
*Source Critical Summary:* `source_md/LAO Practice Note No. 5-2022 (May 2022)_CS.md`
