<a id="pn-5-2024"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 5/2024 — Standard Rates for HSK/HT NDA Land Exchange
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
16 August 2024 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Rates valid through processing to **31 March 2025** | Option under **LAO PN 1/2024**

> Scope note: Optional **standard-rate premium** for lease modification / land exchange of sites in **Hung Shui Kiu / Ha Tsuen NDA**. It is **not** a GB/OZP design code. Processing priority and application deadlines remain those in **PN 1/2024**. Option is **irreversible** at PBTO.

---

## Regulatory Overview

An applicant may opt for standard-rate premium **not later than acceptance of the PBTO** by lodging Appendix I. Second Phase sites must have BBTO (with premium) accepted by **31 March 2025** or processing ceases (Remaining Phase is lower priority; re-application for Second Phase after **30 April 2024** will **not** be considered).

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. Option window and consequences (paras 2, 6–7)

| Rule | Lock |
|---|---|
| Opt by | **Not later than PBTO acceptance** (Option Form App I) |
| After opt accepted | **No appeal** against BBTO standard-rate premium; application fees **forfeited** if the applicant then drops the exchange |
| No Option Form by PBTO | Premium assessed **case-by-case**; dropping the BBTO still **forfeits** fees; **no** Second Phase re-application after **30 April 2024** |
| LandsD discretion | Whether the exchange is eligible for standard rates is **final** |
| Pre-1987 IBs (fn 1) | If the exchange involves eligible pre-1987 IBs under **LAO PN 12/2023**, applicant may opt **only** the rates **in this PN**, not other PNs’ rates |

**SD takeaway:** Decide the standard-rate option **before PBTO** — after that the premium is locked and Second Phase cannot be re-filed after **30 April 2024**.

---

### 2. HSK/HT standard rates (para 3) — valid to 31 March 2025

Agricultural = **per site m²**; other columns = **per GFA m²**.

| NDA | Ag | NR before^ | Res before | NR after# | Res after |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Hung Shui Kiu / Ha Tsuen | $4,500 | $13,500 | $26,500 | $24,000 | $34,000 |

^ Before NR includes commercial and industrial; LandsD’s type call is final.  
# After NR includes commercial and **modern industrial**; **excludes** columbarium, PFS, DG storage, offensive trades; **hotel and ancillary = non-residential**; LandsD’s type call is final.

**SD takeaway:** After-exchange residential GFA prices at **$34,000/m²**; hotel is **non-residential ($24,000/m²)** — split the GFA schedule to match.

---

### 3. Premium formula (para 4): A×B − C×D − E

| Factor | Meaning |
|---|---|
| **A** | Maximum **total GFA after** exchange in the land documents; LandsD’s figure at absolute discretion |
| **B** | After-use rate(s) in para 3; **residential** rate on all GFA **not prohibiting private residential**; **non-residential** rate on the remainder |
| **C** | **Site area of Agricultural land** surrendered and/or **permissible GFA of Building land** surrendered |
| **D** | Before-use rate(s); on Building land, residential rate where residential is **not prohibited** |
| **E** | Deductible **construction cost of Government Accommodation** the applicant must build/hand back/operate for Government; assessed by the proponent department, **non-negotiable**; if not conveyed before the Option deadline, **E = 0** (also on case-by-case assessment) |
| Negative result | **No premium** charged |
| No max total GFA in the exchange document | **This PN does not apply** |

**GFA for A:** floor space **solely as Government Accommodation** may be disregarded. If GA is stated as **NOFA**, convert **NOFA:GFA = 1:1.8** (e.g. 100 m² NOFA = 180 m² GFA). Other floor space is disregarded **only** if **expressly** GFA-exempted in the exchange document.

**Agricultural land (C):** old-schedule and new-grant lots demised for agriculture, plus agricultural portion of mixed lots; assessed on **site area**.

**Building land (C):** land on which building is allowed, plus building portion of mixed lots. Old-schedule lots count as Building land if the Block Government Lease schedule term is one of: house, building, mat shed, boat hut, temple, nunnery, latrine, shop, cow shed, cattle shed, duck shed, cow byre, pigsty, school, or other uses LandsD determines; those old-schedule lots are treated as **residential** Building land (residential not prohibited). Permissible GFA is as specified in the lease; if **none**, plot ratio **1.33** may be adopted. Untraceable leases: use and site area by LandsD.

**SD takeaway:** You need a **stated maximum total GFA** in the exchange, a clean **ag vs building** surrender split, and a **GA NOFA→GFA at 1.8** if Government Accommodation is in the scheme — missing max GFA knocks the site off this PN.

---

### 4. Survey pack for “C” (para 5)

Provide, **not later than settlement of the initial administrative fee**: (i) Survey Submission by an **Authorised Land Surveyor** (Cap. 473) — Survey Record Plan, Land Boundary Plan, Survey Report per the Code of Practice — showing wholly surrendered lots (surveyed and registered areas), partial surrenders, and the regrant site; (ii) **title deeds** of all lots to be surrendered. LandsD then states “C” in a **separate letter at PBTO**. If the pack is not provided and the applicant still opts standard rates, LandsD fixes “C” from information it has and issues BBTO. LandsD’s “C” is **final** and **may differ** from the Block Government Lease.

**SD takeaway:** Commission the **ALS** boundary survey early enough to hit the **initial admin-fee** date — that is what sets the agricultural site area / building GFA credit.

---

### Source

- `PN 5_2024.pdf`
- Read with: **LAO PN 1/2024** (HSK/HT application windows); **LAO PN 12/2023** (pre-1987 IB standard rates — cannot mix with this PN)
*Source Critical Summary:* `source_md/LAO Practice Note No. 5-2024 (August 2024)_CS.md`

<a id="pn-6-2024"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 6/2024 — BIM for Area Calculation on GBP Submissions
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
30 September 2024 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Immediate effect

> Scope note: LandsD **accepts BIM files** for **mathematical area calculations** in GBP submissions under lease, **in addition to** CAD files under **LAO PN 3/2018**. It is **not** a substitute for the GBP itself. Read with **PNAP ADM-19** para 24 and **PNAP ADV-34**, and LandsD **Guidance Notes on Use of BIM for Mathematical Calculation of Areas**.

---

## Regulatory Overview

When BIM is used to demonstrate area calculations, the AP must follow those Guidance Notes. Approval or disapproval under lease is given only to a GBP in hard copy or in the electronic format accepted by BD under **PNAP ADM-17**, and if GBP and BIM disagree the **GBP prevails**.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. How to submit BIM + coloured plans (paras 3, 6)

| Mode | BIM delivery | Also required |
|---|---|---|
| Hardcopy GBP | BIM on **non-rewritable DVD-ROM** diskette(s) | **Coloured Building Plans** (Guidance Notes paras 6–9): **identical** to GBP floor layouts; coloured to show **SC extent, transfer plate, GFA-accountable and non-accountable** areas under lease |
| GBP via **ESH** | Upload BIM **simultaneously** with the electronic copy through ESH | Same Coloured Building Plans |
| Non-compliance | Requirements above **or** Guidance Notes timeframe/technical requirements not met | GBP **may be rejected without further checking** |
| Coverage | Immediate; applies to **all** GBP submissions with BIM, including **re-submissions, major revisions, amendments** | — |

**SD takeaway:** BIM is extra evidence, not the approval drawing — still issue **Coloured Building Plans** that match the GBP, on DVD-ROM (paper) or **simultaneous ESH** upload, or the GBP can be **rejected unread**.

---

### 2. Government reuse of BIM (para 4)

Submission of BIM files is taken as **permitting** the Government to use the models to update the **3-Dimensional Digital Map** and other spatial data, and to keep them in the **Government BIM Data Repository** for smart-city uses (e.g. city planning and urban design).

**SD takeaway:** Assume submitted BIM is **not confidential vis-à-vis Government spatial/BIM repositories** — strip anything the client cannot have reused before issue.

---

- Read with: **LAO PN 3/2018** (CAD area calculation); LandsD **Guidance Notes on Use of BIM for Mathematical Calculation of Areas**; **PNAP ADM-19**, **ADV-34**, **ADM-17**

<a id="pn-8-2024"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 8/2024 — Application for Special Waiver for Conversion of an Entire Existing Industrial Building
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
31 October 2024 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Immediate effect | Varies/supplements LAO PN Nos. 6/2019 and 6/2019A

> Scope note: Extends the **entire-IB conversion special-waiver** window and **drops the 10% Designated Portion** for **pre-1987 IBs** only. Other IBs aged 15+ still carry the 10% Specified Uses lock in PN 6/2019 para 3(a). Parent PNs otherwise remain valid.

---

## Regulatory Overview

This PN, together with LAO PN Nos. 6/2019 and 6/2019A (collectively "the PNs"), governs special waivers to convert an entire existing industrial building. LandsD considers each application on its merits as landlord; the PNs create no expectation of approval.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. Application deadline (para 2)

| Item | Lock |
|---|---|
| Former deadline (PN 6/2019A para 2) | On or before **31 October 2024** |
| New deadline | On or before **31 December 2027** |

**SD takeaway:** Entire-IB conversion special-waiver applications under the PNs must be **received** by LandsD on or before **31 December 2027**.

---

### 2. Pre-1987 IB — 10% Designated Portion dropped (paras 3–4)

A **pre-1987 IB** is a building on an industrial lot (as defined in footnote 2 of **LAO PN 2/2019**) that was wholly or partly constructed **on or before 1 March 1987**, or constructed to building plans **first submitted** to BA on or before that date.

| Building | If special waiver approved |
|---|---|
| **Pre-1987 IB**, nil waiver fee, under PN 6/2019 & 6/2019A | Condition to designate, reserve and restrict **not less than 10%** of total accountable GFA (BO) of the existing IB after conversion ("Designated Portion") for Specified Uses — **dispensed with**, including associated provisions in PN 6/2019 para 3(a) |
| Entire existing IB **other than** a pre-1987 IB, aged **15 years or more** | **Still** must designate **≥ 10%** of total accountable GFA for Specified Uses after conversion (PN 6/2019 para 3(a)) |

On-going pre-1987 applications received **before** this PN, where the special waiver is **not yet approved/granted**: applicant may submit **revised conversion-works proposals** and revised schematic parking-floor plans (if needed) deleting the Designated Portion — **not** treated as a new application. If the IB is the subject of a TPB approval, obtain the necessary **Town Planning Ordinance** approvals and lodge the TPB approval letter for the revised (no-Designated-Portion) scheme.

**SD takeaway:** For a pre-1987 entire-IB conversion, do **not** bank 10% GFA as Specified Uses — reclaim it into the conversion brief; non-pre-1987 IBs ≥15 years still need that 10% lock.

---

### 3. Ineligible stock and form (paras 5–6)

| Lock | Detail |
|---|---|
| Sample form | Appendix I to this PN **supersedes** Appendix I to PN 6/2019 |
| Not eligible | IBs that have **completed redevelopment** under IB revitalisation in **PN 1/2010, 1/2010A, 1/2010B** or **PN 2/2019, 2/2019A** |

**SD takeaway:** A lot already redeveloped under the 2010 or 2019 IB revitalisation schemes cannot take a PN 6/2019 entire-building conversion waiver.

---

### Source

<a id="pn-1-2025"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 1/2025 — Self-certification of compliance for NTEHs
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
January 2025 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Pilot: DLO Yuen Long, **9 months** from 23 January 2025 (later extended by PN 1/2025B)

> Scope note: Optional **self-certification of compliance (SCC)** for **Certificate of Compliance** / **No Objection to Occupy** on Cap. 121 **New Territories Exempted Houses**. It is **not** a BO OP process and does **not** replace CoE for building/site formation/drainage works.

---

## Regulatory Overview

The PN applies only where a CoE for **building works** has been issued under Cap. 121 for the NTEH (site-formation/drainage CoEs need not also have been issued). DLO may, when issuing the land document, Approval Letter or CoE, rule the case **unsuitable** for SCC and force the conventional inspection path.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. Which applications (paras 2–4)

| Track | Typical locks in the land document / Approval Letter |
|---|---|
| New village house (land document) | Erect a building; **non-industrial** user; CoE required before BW/SF/drainage; **no occupation** until DLO CC |
| Rebuild (Approval Letter) | Non-industrial; CoE before works; DLO may issue **No Objection to Occupy** when Approval Letter conditions are met |
| In scope | CC under 2(d) **or** No Objection letter under 3(c), with building-works CoE |

**SD takeaway:** SCC is only for Cap. 121 NTEHs that already have a building-works CoE — conventional houses under Cap. 123 use PN 1/2026, not this PN.

---

### 2. Who may certify (para 6 and fn 2–3)

| Item | Lock |
|---|---|
| Registered professional (RP) | **RGE**, **RSE**, **RPE** (relevant discipline), or **AP** (architect / engineer / surveyor) as defined in Cap. 123 |
| Independence | RP submitting SCC **must not be or affiliated with** the person submitting Construction Completion Report (Forms CE/4 and CE/5) |
| Non-standard NTEH features | Seek **preliminary DLO advice** before CC application — standard forms cover usual features only |

**SD takeaway:** Appoint an independent RP (not the CE/4–CE/5 author) at SD if the village house will use the 10-week SCC stream.

---

### 3. Requisite Documents (para 6)

| Item | Content |
|---|---|
| Form 1 + App II + App III | SCC form; Standard Report on completed NTEH; Construction Completion Report |
| Ownership | Computer printout of current ownership |
| Form 2 (App IV) | Owner’s Statement |
| Others as required | Site plan(s), on-site photos, etc. |

**SD takeaway:** Build the as-built photo/plan pack into the completion programme — missing Requisite Documents stop the 2-week validity clock.

---

### 4. Processing times (paras 8–11)

| Step | Target |
|---|---|
| Valid application? | DLO decides within **2 weeks** of **all** Requisite Documents (no separate “valid” notice) |
| Invalid RP (not on Cap. 123 / Cap. 409 register) | Falls back to **conventional** processing |
| Streamlined CC | LandsD target: **10 weeks** from complete documents → CC **or** reasoned refusal |
| **Audit** selection (fn 5: then processed conventionally) | Clock extends to **14 weeks**; applicant is told |
| Pre-PN applications opting in | Processing **withheld** until Requisite Documents arrive; priority from **actual receipt date** |

**SD takeaway:** Programme NTEH occupation around **10 weeks** (14 if audited) from a complete SCC pack — not from first incomplete lodging.

---

### 5. After CC (para 12)

Issuance does not prejudice Government enforcement. Misconduct on SCC (including RP misconduct) may be referred to the Building Authority, professional bodies and other authorities.

**SD takeaway:** SCC is not a safe-harbour if the house does not match the certified parameters.

---

### Source

- `PN 1_2025.pdf`
- Varied by: **LAO PN No. 1/2025B** (all NT DLOs from 24 November 2025). Read with Cap. 121 CoE practice.
*Source Critical Summary:* `source_md/LAO Practice Note No. 1-2025 (January 2025)_CS.md`

**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
November 2025 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Immediate effect | Varies PN 1/2025; **supersedes PN 1/2025A**

> Scope note: Changes **where** the NTEH Certificate of Compliance / No-Objection-to-Occupy self-certification stream runs. It does **not** rewrite the SCC forms, RP rules, or 10/14-week targets in PN 1/2025.

## Regulatory Overview

Paragraph 13 of PN 1/2025 is replaced. All other provisions of PN 1/2025 remain valid.

---

|---|---|
| DLO Yuen Long from **23 January 2025** | Pilot |
| DLO North from **9 July 2025** | Pilot |
| **All** District Lands Offices, New Territories from **24 November 2025** | Streamlined arrangement is the **standard procedure** |

**SD takeaway:** From 24 November 2025, NTEH CC/No-Objection applications in **every NT DLO** may use the PN 1/2025 self-certification stream (subject to DLO suitability) — no longer a Yuen Long/North-only pilot.

<a id="pn-2-2025"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 2/2025 — Yuen Long South Second Phase land exchange
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
February 2025 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Read with **LAO PN No. 13/2023** (ECNTA)

> Scope note: Lease modification / in-situ land exchange for **two** designated **OU(Storage and Workshop)** sites in Yuen Long South NDA Second Phase. **PN 12/2023 / 12/2023A** (IB standard rates) and **PN 11/2023 / 11/2023A** (ag-land pilot standard rates) **do not apply**. NDA standard-rate premium “to be announced”.

---

## Regulatory Overview

Follow approved Tong Yan San Tsuen OZP **S/YL-TYST/14** (20 August 2021 or later amendments) and the Revised Recommended ODP (yls.hk). Only Site A (~3.7 ha, wholly OU(SW)) and Site B (~1.2 ha, largely OU(SW) plus a ‘Road’ strip now intended for storage/workshop) on Reference Plan App I are open. OZP Notes: OU(SW) is for **multi-storey buildings** housing brownfield operations that cannot sit in conventional flatted factories (bulky/heavy goods, high headroom).

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. The two sites (para 3 and fn 2)

| Site | Planning lock |
|---|---|
| **A** ~3.7 ha | Entirely **OU(SW)** |
| **B** ~1.2 ha | Mostly OU(SW); remainder shown as **‘Road’** on OZP — Government intends the **extended** site as OU(SW); Government will seek **TPB permission** for storage/workshop on the Road portion **if** an in-situ exchange is received. Expected ~**six calendar months** before BBTO; if permission is late, Government may **reject** or handle otherwise |
| Boundaries | Illustration only; Government may adjust |

**SD takeaway:** Design both sites as high-headroom multi-storey workshop; Site B’s Road strip is not a public road in the exchange scheme but needs TPB permission on Government’s application.

---

### 2. Government Accommodation on Site A (para 6)

| Item | Lock |
|---|---|
| Site A only (larger site) | On completion, hand over **20% of floor space** as **Government Accommodation** for displaced brownfield operators |
| Use of the 20% | Government lets it; uses **may differ** from the remaining 80% (cluster/synergy considered) |
| Site B | This invitation’s 20% GA rule is stated for Site A |

**SD takeaway:** On Site A freeze **20% GFA as GA** with independent letting/services; the 80% private workshop can be a different operator mix.

---

### 3. Public facilities and 90% ownership (para 5, App IV)

| Item | Lock |
|---|---|
| Relaxed ownership (PN 13/2023 paras 8–10) | Applicant **shall** construct specified public facilities |
| Other applicants | Government **may** invite; construction cost **may** be premium-deductible |
| Assembly | Surrender **all** private land in the designated site (minor layout-neutral boundary tweaks OK). Planning intention = **one development per site**. **90%+** → relaxed criterion. Re-grant conforms to designated site; **GL not promised** |
| Access | Vehicular access feasible; no adverse impact on adjacent **vehicular and/or pedestrian** access |
| OZP | Use and parameters comply |

**SD takeaway:** These two sites are full-assembly (or 90%) single developments — no 4 000 m² residential carve-out option.

---

### 4. Deadlines (para 8)

| Item | Date |
|---|---|
| Submit applications | **19 May 2025** |
| Accepted application: BBTO with premium issued **and** accepted | **19 May 2026** or processing ceases (fees not refunded) |
| Lodge at | CES/NDA, Landmark North, Sheung Shui |

**SD takeaway:** Workshop scheme and 20% GA package must be BBTO-ready by 19 May 2026.

---

### 5. Submission pack (App II) — design-facing

Must: letter with proposed user, **max GFA, SC, storeys/BH**; 1:1000 plan; TPB letter if any; ownership printout.

Facilitate: OZP extract; assignment/Deed Poll plans; sketches; MLP / EIA / TIA / DIA; occupant schedules (current **and** occupants at **10 July 2020** or after who left); programme; ALS survey to prove **90%** private-land threshold.

**SD takeaway:** First pack needs GFA/SC/BH, 90% survey, and the 10 July 2020 occupant trail.

---

## LAO Practice Note No. 4/2025 — Standard Rates for NT Agricultural Land Exchange (outside NDAs)
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
10 April 2025 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Rates effective **1 April 2025** | Varies/supplements **LAO PN 11/2023A**

> Scope note: **Rate and Option Form update** for the pilot scheme charging land premium at **standard rates** on lease modification / land exchange for **agricultural land in the New Territories outside NDAs**. It is **not** an NDA land-exchange PN and **not** a BO GFA code. Parent processing rules in **PN 11/2023A** (and **PN 11/2023** para 4 zones) otherwise remain valid.

---

## Regulatory Overview

From **1 April 2025**, standard rates for those exchanges follow the **zones in paragraph 4 of LAO PN 11/2023** where the lot(s) sit (agricultural-use rates **per site area**; all other listed rates **per GFA**). The Option Form in PN 11/2023A Appendix I is **replaced** by Appendix I to this PN.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. How zones and uses are applied (para 2 notes)

| Rule | Lock |
|---|---|
| Overlapping zones | **Higher** rates apply |
| Site partly in / partly out of a zone | **Whole site** eligible for standard rates; LandsD’s rates for the out-of-zone part are **final** |
| Site straddling two zones/sub-zones | Respective rates applied to **GFA after exchange on a pro-rata** of site area in each zone/sub-zone, assuming GFA **evenly distributed** |
| After-exchange **residential** rate | All GFA **not prohibiting residential use** |
| After-exchange **non-residential** rate | Remainder GFA; **not** applicable to **columbarium, petrol filling station, dangerous-goods storage, or offensive trades**; **hotel and ancillary** = non-residential |
| Before-exchange non-residential | Includes commercial and industrial; LandsD’s classification is **final** |
| New town (**#**) | OZP new town **plus fringe generally within 1 km** of its railway station(s); LandsD’s 1 km call is **final** |
| Railway catchment | Generally within **1 km** of the relevant station; LandsD’s 1 km call is **final** |
| Option | Irreversible on **PBTO** acceptance; **no premium appeal** at BBTO; fees **forfeited** if the applicant then drops the deal; completed standard-rate deals **published** on LandsD’s website |

**SD takeaway:** Freeze **zone (and sub-zone)** and **residential vs non-residential GFA split** before opting — straddling sites get **pro-rata GFA**, overlapping zones take the **higher** rate, and hotel GFA is **non-residential**.

---

### 2. Standard rates from 1 April 2025 (para 2 table)

Columns: agricultural (per **site m²**) | non-res before | residential before | non-res after | residential after (after columns per **GFA m²**).

| No. | District / zone | Ag | NR before | Res before | NR after | Res after |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **#** Yuen Long Town | $5,000 | $12,500 | $22,000 | $19,000 | $28,000 |
| 2 | Hung Shui Kiu Station | $5,000 | $12,500 | $22,000 | $18,000 | $27,000 |
| 3 | Kam Sheung Road Station | $5,000 | $12,500 | $22,000 | $16,000 | $26,000 |
| 4 | **#** Tin Shui Wai Town | $5,000 | $12,500 | $22,000 | $16,000 | $26,000 |
| 5 | San Tin Station | $5,000 | $9,000 | $17,500 | $14,000 | $23,000 |
| 6 | Ngau Tam Mei Station | $5,000 | $9,000 | $17,500 | $14,000 | $23,000 |
| 7 | Au Tau Station | $5,000 | $9,500 | $18,500 | $15,000 | $24,000 |
| 8 | **#** Fanling / Sheung Shui North Town | $5,000 | $12,500 | $22,000 | $18,000 | $27,000 |
| 9 | Kwu Tung Station | $5,000 | $12,500 | $22,000 | $18,000 | $27,000 |
| 10(a) | **#** Tuen Mun Town **Sub-zone 1** | $5,000 | $12,500 | $22,000 | $16,000 | $26,000 |
| 10(b) | **#** Tuen Mun Town **Sub-zone 2** | $5,000 | $9,500 | $17,000 | $12,000 | $22,000 |

Agricultural rate is **$5,000/m² site** in every listed zone. **#** = new town (OZP + generally 1 km station fringe). Station rows are railway catchment / proposed-station zones as marked in the PN.

**SD takeaway:** Agricultural “before” value is **site area × $5,000**; “after” value is **GFA ×** the zone’s res/NR rate — Tuen Mun **sub-zone** is a real split ($26,000 vs $22,000 residential after).

---

<a id="pn-5-2025"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 5/2025 — Standard Rates for Industrial Building Redevelopment
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
10 April 2025 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Immediate effect | Varies/supplements **LAO PN 12/2023**; **supersedes** **LAO PN 12/2023A**  
Rates from **1 April 2025**

> Scope note: **Rate and Option Form update** plus a restated **“B” (after-use rate)** rule for standard-rate premium on lease modification / in-situ exchange for **redevelopment of industrial buildings** on industrial lots. Parent processing in **PN 12/2023** otherwise remains valid. Five regions as delineated in **Appendix II to PN 12/2023**.

---

## Regulatory Overview

Paragraph 5(ii) of PN 12/2023 (factor **B**) is rewritten so that **residential** rates apply to applications that do **not prohibit private residential**, except on **CDA** or **OU (commercial-cum-residential)** OZP/planning-approval sites, where residential rate applies only to GFA not prohibiting private residential and **commercial** (or **modern industrial**, if so specified in the modification) applies to the remainder. **Hotel and hotel-ancillary GFA = commercial**; standard rates from **1 April 2025** are tabulated below.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. After-use rate “B” (varied para 5(ii) of PN 12/2023)

| Site / GFA | Rate to use |
|---|---|
| Application **not prohibiting private residential** (general case) | **Residential** rate |
| OZP and/or planning approval is **CDA** or **OU for commercial-cum-residential** | **Residential** rate on GFA **not prohibiting private residential**; **commercial** rate on remainder, **except** GFA specified as **modern industrial** in the modification → **modern industrial** rate |
| Hotel + hotel ancillary GFA | **Commercial** |

LandsD’s decision on use type and which rate attaches to any GFA is **final**.

**SD takeaway:** On a **CDA / OU C/R** IB redevelopment, split GFA into private-residential vs commercial vs modern-industrial in the modification documents — hotel counts as **commercial**, not residential.

---

### 2. Standard rates from 1 April 2025 (para 3) — $/m²

Regions = Appendix II to **PN 12/2023**. Before = Industrial/Godown (except Special Industrial) | Special Industrial. After = Modern Industrial | Commercial | Residential.

| Region | Ind/Godown | Special Ind | Modern Ind | Commercial | Residential |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Hong Kong Island | 26,500 | 18,500 | 43,000 | 48,000 | 75,000 |
| Kowloon East | 26,500 | 18,500 | 37,000 | 41,000 | 56,000 |
| Kowloon West | 23,500 | 16,500 | 36,000 | 40,000 | 62,000 |
| New Territories South | 23,500 | 16,500 | 30,000 | 35,000 | 41,000 |
| New Territories North | 14,000 | 10,000 | 17,000 | 20,000 | 29,000 |

**SD takeaway:** HK Island residential redevelopment prices **after** GFA at **$75,000/m²**; NTN at **$29,000/m²** — confirm the **PN 12/2023 Appendix II** region before locking use mix.

---

### 3. Option Form (replaces App I to PN 12/2023)

| Requirement | Lock |
|---|---|
| Pre-1987 IB proof | **OP(s)** for the pre-1987 IB(s); **or** BA approval letter(s) showing building plans were **first submitted on or before 1 March 1987** |
| GFA of existing building (#) | **AP or RPS** certificate of GFA of the pre-1987 IB existing on the lot(s) or existing immediately before vacancy, per **latest BA-approved plans**, **excluding** parking, L/UL, plant rooms and similar **not normally GFA-accountable** under the BO, and any GFA on those plans that **does not comply with lease**; attach the approved plans |
| Alternative “C” | Applicant may skip the Certificate and adopt GFA as if the site were redeveloped for **industrial and/or godown** (the case-by-case basis in PN 12/2023 para 5(iii)); that choice is **irreversible**; LandsD’s figure is **final** |
| If opt accepted | **No BBTO premium appeal**; fees **forfeited** if the applicant then drops; completed standard-rate deals **published** on LandsD’s website |

**SD takeaway:** Either certify **existing IB GFA** (net of typical non-accountable areas and lease-breaching GFA) or **irreversibly** take the industrial-redevelopment “C” — have the OP / 1 March 1987 first-submission proof in the option pack.

---

<a id="pn-6-2025"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 6/2025 — Standard Rates for JPN 1 & 2 Balcony / UP / NSPEWall Premium
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
10 April 2025 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Rates effective **1 April 2025**

> Scope note: **Rate revision only** for premium on lease exemption of balconies, utility platforms and NSPEWalls from GFA and SC under **JPN 1** and **JPN 2**. Varies/supplements LAO PN **3/2001** (as supplemented by **6/2001**), **6/2002**, **3/2003**, **2/2011**, **4/2023** and **3/2024**; other provisions in those PNs remain valid. Districts follow **Appendix VII to LAO PN 3/2024** (five regions). It does **not** restate JPN dimensional rules.

---

## Regulatory Overview

From **1 April 2025** the standard premium rates for those exemptions are revised. Which appendix applies still depends on the **JPN version** on the lease (October 2004 vs January 2011 JPN 1; February 2006 vs January 2011 JPN 2, or later variations); a lot straddling two districts uses the **higher** rate.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. Which table / which JPN vintage (para 2)

| Feature | JPN vintage | Appendix |
|---|---|---|
| Balcony | JPN 1 **October 2004** | **I** — per unit by total exempted balcony m² (2.0–5.0) |
| Balcony | JPN 1 **January 2011** | **II** — per unit by total exempted balcony m² (1.0–3.0) |
| Utility platform | JPN 2 **February 2006** | **III** — flat premium per residential unit |
| Utility platform | JPN 2 **January 2011** | **IV** — flat premium per residential unit |
| NSPEWall | JPN 2 **February 2006** | **V** — $/m² of BA-exempted area; thickness **> 300 mm** is **GFA-accountable** |
| NSPEWall | JPN 2 **January 2011** | **VI** — same method; thickness **> 150 mm** is **GFA-accountable** |

| Cross-cutting rule | Lock |
|---|---|
| District map | **Appendix VII to LAO PN 3/2024** (Hong Kong Island / Kowloon East / Kowloon West / NT South / NT North) |
| Straddling lots | **Higher** rate |
| Balcony rounding | AP to nearest **0.001 m²**; **2.544 → 2.5 m²**; **2.545 → 2.6 m²** |

**SD takeaway:** Use **PN 3/2024 Appendix VII** regions (not the old six-DLO map in PN 4/2023) and the lease’s JPN vintage — 2011 NSPEWall still caps exemption at **150 mm**.

---

### 2. Balcony — representative per-unit premiums from 1.4.2025 (App I–II)

**October 2004 JPN 1 (App I)** — premium per unit (HK$):

| Exempted balcony m²/unit | HK Island | Kowloon East | Kowloon West | NT South | NT North |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2.0 | 70,500 | 52,700 | 58,300 | 36,100 | 22,100 |
| 3.0 | 135,500 | 101,600 | 112,300 | 49,800 | 33,400 |
| 5.0 | 305,800 | 229,600 | 254,000 | 87,600 | 63,000 |

**January 2011 JPN 1 (App II)** — premium per unit (HK$):

| Exempted balcony m²/unit | HK Island | Kowloon East | Kowloon West | NT South | NT North |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1.0 | 24,700 | 18,500 | 20,400 | 10,000 | 2,800 |
| 2.0 | 80,800 | 61,200 | 66,700 | 28,500 | 29,400 |
| 3.0 | 158,800 | 120,500 | 131,200 | 46,300 | 50,100 |

Full 0.1 m² steps are in the appendices.

**SD takeaway:** Budget balcony premium **per flat** on the five-region map — HK Island at 5.0 m² on the 2004 table is **$305,800**/unit.

---

### 3. Utility platform — standard premium per residential unit from 1.4.2025 (App III–IV)

| JPN 2 version | HK Island | Kowloon East | Kowloon West | NT South | NT North |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Feb 2006** | $52,880 | $39,530 | $43,730 | $27,080 | $16,580 |
| **Jan 2011** | $18,530 | $13,880 | $15,300 | $7,500 | $2,100 |

**SD takeaway:** UP remains a **flat per-unit** charge; 2011 vintage is substantially cheaper (HK Island **$18,530** vs **$52,880**).

---

### 4. NSPEWall — $/m² of BA-exempted area from 1.4.2025 (App V–VI)

Thickness from **approved building plans**; multiple thicknesses → **majority** principle. Average flat size = total residential GFA / total residential units; split at **104 m²(G)**. Mosaic/similar **tile cladding** that only beautifies is **not** in the premium calc; **curtain-wall-type** cladding on structural walls is GFA-countable unless exempted by lease modification + assessed premium.

**130 mm or less** (App V Feb 2006 and App VI Jan 2011), HK$/m²:

| District | ≤ 104 m²(G) | > 104 m²(G) |
|---|---|---|
| Hong Kong Island | 75,000 | 115,410 |
| Kowloon East | 56,000 | 86,170 |
| Kowloon West | 62,000 | 95,400 |
| New Territories South | 41,000 | 39,240 |
| New Territories North | 29,000 | 27,510 |

App V continues in 10 mm steps to **290–300 mm**. App VI (Jan 2011) stops at **140–150 mm**.

**SD takeaway:** Keep NSPEWall **≤ 150 mm** on 2011 leases (or **≤ 300 mm** on 2006) or the excess is **GFA**; decorative tile cladding stays out of the premium area.

---

<a id="pn-7-2025"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 7/2025 — Standard Rates for Premium Calculation for Exemption of GFA and/or Site Coverage and Relaxation of Building Height Restriction arising from Modular Integrated Construction under Joint Practice Note No. 8
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
10 April 2025 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Effective **1 April 2025** | Varies/supplements LAO PN Nos. 6/2022 and 4/2024

> Scope note: Replaces the **MiC GFA/SC exemption and BHR-relaxation premium rates** in Appendix I to **PN 4/2024**. It is **not** JPN 8 itself — JPN 8 still governs when BA disregards MiC floor area. Parent PNs remain valid except the rates table.

---

## Regulatory Overview

From 1 April 2025, premium for lease exemption of GFA and/or site coverage and relaxation of building height restriction arising from Modular Integrated Construction (JPN 8) is calculated using Appendix I to this PN instead of Appendix I to PN 4/2024. All other provisions of PN 6/2022 and PN 4/2024 remain valid.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. How premium is calculated (Appendix I notes)

| Rule | Lock |
|---|---|
| Districts | **Hong Kong Island, Kowloon East, Kowloon West, New Territories South, New Territories North** — boundaries follow **Appendix II to LAO PN 4/2024** |
| Straddling lots | **Higher** of the two district rates |
| Rate basis | Unit rate per **exempted MiC floor area (m²)** |
| Quantum | Rate × exempted MiC floor area **approved by the Building Authority** |
| Rounding | Exempted MiC floor area(s) **rounded up to nearest 0.1 m²** |
| Negotiation | Premium determined by LandsD at absolute discretion; **not negotiable** |
| Domestic vs non-domestic | LandsD has absolute discretion on which rate type applies, per nature of development and the lease. If BA-approved GBPs have **not** specified whether exempted MiC floor area is domestic or non-domestic GFA, the **domestic** standard rate is used |

Appendix I also tabulates separate **Domestic** and **Non-Domestic** unit rates ($/m²) for the five districts. The extract’s grid is OCR-scrambled; use `PN 7_2025.pdf` Appendix I for the cell values (rates printed include $7,500, $6,200, $5,600, $4,800, $4,100, $4,000, $3,500, $2,900 and $2,000 per m²).

**SD takeaway:** Freeze BA-approved exempted MiC m² (domestic vs non-domestic tagged on GBP) and the PN 4/2024 district before quoting premium — LandsD will round up to 0.1 m² and default to the **domestic** rate if the GBP is silent.

---

### Source

- `PN 7_2025.pdf` (LAO PN No. 7/2025, 10 April 2025)
- Read with: **JPN No. 8**; **LAO PN 6/2022** (parent MiC lease process); **LAO PN 4/2024** (five-region map in Appendix II; rates replaced).
*Source Critical Summary:* `source_md/LAO Practice Note No. 7_2025 (April 2025)_CS.md`

<a id="pn-8-2025"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 8/2025 — Streamlined Arrangement on Phased GBP, DDH/DD and Landscaping Proposal Approval for Residential Units in Uncompleted Developments
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
17 April 2025 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Immediate effect (paras 3–5)

> Scope note: Lets LandsD issue **phased** general building plan, DDH/DD, and landscaping approvals under lease so a **Cap. 621 "phase"** can go to pre-sale without whole-lot GBP/DDH. Other LACO CM 72F whole-lot approvals (MLP, carpark layout, DMC, etc.) still bind pre-sale. It is **not** a BO phasing code.

---

## Regulatory Overview

Until this PN, GBP, DDH/DD and landscaping approvals under lease were issued on a **whole-lot** basis even for phased developments. LandsD will now consider phased approvals covering a specific part of the development that is a **"phase"** as defined in the Residential Properties (First-hand Sales) Ordinance (Cap. 621). "Lease" includes Government Lease or Conditions of Grant/Sale/Exchange.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. Phased GBP and phased DDH/DD (paras 3–4)

| Lock | Detail |
|---|---|
| Who applies | **Authorised Person** for the applicant |
| When | At submission to LandsD of the **full set of up-to-date GBP at stage 2** of the Streamlined Building Plans Checking Process (**LAO PN 4/2018**) |
| Must attach | **Phasing Plans** approved by BA under **s.14(1) BO (Cap. 123)** |
| Purpose | Expedite residential units in a phased development; facilitate **pre-sale consent for part** of the development; leave some lease requirements to a later stage |

Conditions LandsD may impose include:

| (a) | Clear **delineation** in the Phasing Plans of the phase under application; Phasing Plans fulfil corresponding **lease** requirements |
| (b) | All relevant **lease requirements of that phase** complied with to LandsD's satisfaction — e.g. Consent Letter for exemption of **balconies and utility platforms** from GFA and/or SC; Consent Letter allowing BA-exempted **MiC** floor area to be exempted from GFA and/or SC |
| (c) | **AP certification** that there is **no insurmountable problem** for the applicant to design the development to comply with lease requirements for the **remaining phase(s)** |

**SD takeaway:** Draw Cap. 621 phases so the first-sale phase is lease-complete on its own (balcony/UP and MiC consent letters in hand) and the AP can certify the remainder is designable — otherwise LandsD will not phase the GBP/DDH.

---

### 2. Phased landscaping and residual whole-lot approvals (paras 5–6)

| Item | Lock |
|---|---|
| Phased landscaping | Considered **only if** phased GBP or phased DDH/DD has been issued; **case-by-case** |
| Planning-condition landscaping | Where another department must approve landscaping **before** LandsD's phased landscaping approval (e.g. planning condition), LandsD **deems** phased landscaping approved for **pre-sale consent** once that other department has approved the **phased** landscaping proposal |
| Still whole-lot | After phased GBP / DDH/DD / landscaping, pre-sale for the phase still needs the other lease approvals in **paras 8.1 and 8.2 of Section III of the Annex to LACO CM No. 72F** (issued the same day), which remain **whole-lot** |

Para 2 of this PN still cites **LACO CM 72E** for the pre-sale list (DDH, MLP, landscaping, carpark layout, natural terrain hazard plans, DMC & MA, etc.).

**SD takeaway:** Phase the GBP/landscape for the sales phase, but programme **whole-lot** MLP, carpark layout, DMC and the rest of CM 72F — pre-sale will not wait on a partial DMC.

---

### Source

- `PN 8_2025.pdf` (LAO PN No. 8/2025, 17 April 2025)
- Read with: **LAO PN 4/2018** (stage 2 GBP); **LACO CM 72E / 72F** (pre-sale approval list); **Cap. 621** (definition of phase); **BO s.14(1)** (BA Phasing Plans).
*Source Critical Summary:* `source_md/LAO Practice Note No. 8_2025 (April 2025)_CS.md`

<a id="pn-9-2025"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 9/2025 — Gross Floor Area Exemption Arrangement for Aboveground Parking
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
4 November 2025 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Immediate effect | Supersedes LAO PN Issue No. 3/2025

> Scope note: Aligns **lease GFA** with BD's **PNAP APP-2** (Nov 2025) disregard of **aboveground** parking spaces and car-park ancillary areas. Applies to **new building plans (including major revision) for new developments submitted to BA on or after 1 November 2025** — **not** existing buildings. It is **not** a substitute for PNAP APP-2; BA still decides BO GFA.

---

## Regulatory Overview

PNAP APP-2 paras 15–18 and Appendix C let BA, under normal circumstances, 100% disregard **not more than two floors** of Aboveground Parking Spaces; in a **site-constraint** case (split levels / stacked multi-level parking because the site cannot be fully utilised), more than two aboveground carparking floors may be 100% disregarded if the **total floor area of the aboveground carpark does not exceed twice the site area**. This PN sets how that exemption is effected **under lease**, by lease type.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. BA envelope this PN rides on (para 1, fn 1–2)

| Item | Lock |
|---|---|
| Ancillary areas (PNAP APP-2 App. C fn 5) | Reasonable-size driveways, ramps, lift shafts and lobbies of car/passenger lifts, and staircases serving the parking spaces — may be disregarded from GFA |
| Normal BA rule | **≤ two floors** of Aboveground Parking Spaces **100%** disregarded |
| Site-constraint BA rule | More than two aboveground carparking floors **100%** disregarded **if** total aboveground carpark floor area **≤ 2 × site area** |

**SD takeaway:** Design aboveground parking to **two full floors** (or, if the site cannot take two full floors, keep total aboveground carpark ≤ **2 × site area**) — that is the BA envelope LandsD will follow.

---

### 2. New land-sale leases (para 2(a))

Provisions will be **in the lease**; exemption is granted at **building-plan checking**; **no** separate LandsD application, **no** premium, **no** administrative fee.

| Lease GFA treatment | What |
|---|---|
| **100% exempt** | Parking **below ground** + ancillary; **and** Aboveground Parking Spaces on **any two floors** + ancillary |
| **100% exempt** (Director's written approval) | Aboveground Parking Spaces on **Additional Floors** (floors other than those two) + ancillary, **if** BA has 100% disregarded them |
| **50% exempt** | Any other Aboveground Parking Spaces + ancillary (not in the two rows above) |

**SD takeaway:** On a **new sale site**, two aboveground parking floors are 100% lease-exempt by the SC; extra floors need BA 100% disregard **plus** Director's written approval — still **nil** premium at plan-check.

---

### 3. Other new leases and lease modifications (not minor/technical) (para 2(b))

| Built into lease / modification letter | Extra (sub-clause) |
|---|---|
| **100%** below-ground parking + ancillary, **and** Aboveground Parking Spaces on **any two floors** + ancillary | Additional Floors 100% disregarded by BA → Director **may** grant 100% lease exemption, and **may require premium and administrative fee** |
| **50%** any other aboveground parking + ancillary | — |

| Site-constraint carve-out (para 2(b)(iii)) | If total aboveground parking + ancillary **≤ 2 × site area** and BA 100% disregards **more than two floors**, premium and administrative fee **may generally not be charged** for Director's written approval of 100% exemption of those Additional Floors |
| Subsequent increase (para 2(b)(v)) | If later GFA of Additional Floors (lease basis) **exceeds Specified Exempted GFA** in the approval letter → further approval, which may attract **additional** premium and admin fee |

At GBP stage, apply to LandsD for full exclusion of Additional Floors; **Specified Exempted GFA** is stipulated in the approval letter (difference between lease-accountable GFA before and after the Director's approval — e.g. moving from 50% accountable to 100% exempt ⇒ Specified Exempted GFA = **50%** of that floor area).

**SD takeaway:** On modification/new grant (not a sale site), budget **premium** for Additional Floors unless you stay inside the **≤ 2 × site area** site-constraint envelope; freeze Specified Exempted GFA and do not grow the carpark past it without a further application.

---

### 4. Public parking vs private; existing leases (paras 2(c)–2(e))

| Lease situation | Path |
|---|---|
| **(c) Public vehicle park** in a private development | Paras 2(a)–(b) apply to **private (ancillary)** parking; **may** apply to **public** parking on **PlanD advice** at lease preparation. If public parking is **100% GFA-accountable under the OZP** (above, at or below ground), the lease/modification will **explicitly** match that OZP accountability |
| **(d) Existing lease** with Appendix I Version 1 or 2 GFA-exemption SCs, **or** a flexibility SC (Appendix II) letting the Director exclude BA-excluded floor space subject to premium | Apply for **approval/consent letter** to exclude aboveground parking that is lease-accountable (full or 50%) but BA-100% disregarded. **Premium + admin fee** on Specified Exempted GFA. **Version 2:** unless BA opines that **at least two floors below ground fully utilising the lot** are provided for parking and L/UL, **all** Aboveground Parking Spaces are taken as **50% GFA-accountable** for the "before" case |
| **(e) Existing lease** with **no** such GFA-exemption provisions | Apply for **lease modification** to add an Appendix II-style clause **plus** approval/consent letter. Modification: **nil premium**; approval/consent: **premium** on Specified Exempted GFA; **one** administrative fee covers both. Later GFA growth: **no** further modification — further approval/consent with premium/admin fee |

**SD takeaway:** Match the parking GFA story to the **existing SC**: Version 2 lots should provide **two fully utilised basement parking/LUL floors** if you want a better "before" GFA; OZP-accountable public parks stay lease-accountable regardless of level.

---

### 5. Premium, admin fee, fast-track (paras 2(f), 3–4; Appendix III–IV)

| Item | Lock |
|---|---|
| Evidence | AP-certified GFA calcs of the aboveground parking + ancillary on the **lease** accountability basis (ancillary generally follows PNAP APP-2 App. C paras 9 and 12; BD's ruling generally followed). Copy GFA calcs to LandsD **BPU** |
| **Private** parking only | Standard rate **$8,900/m²** of Specified Exempted GFA (round up to **0.1 m²**) + admin fee **$90,750** (subject to review). Rate not reviewed **earlier than 1 November 2026**. Rate **prevailing at assessment** applies; **non-negotiable** |
| **Public** parking, or **mix** of public + private | Premium and admin fee **case-by-case** (existing mechanism) — standard rate **does not** apply |
| Fast-track aim | **10 weeks** (private only) / **16 weeks** (public or mix) from a valid application. Apply **after** BA approves the GBP for the GFA Exemption Arrangement |
| **Out of scope** | Fast-track and Appendix III rate **do not** apply if the modification also varies **other** SCs (including excluding **below-ground** private/public parking from total GFA) — then conventional holistic premium |

Checklist (Appendix IV): current ownership printout; agent authorisation; for approval/consent — BA-approved GBPs delineating the spaces + AP GFA calcs; for modification — application letter + 1:1000 location plan.

**SD takeaway:** Keep the application to **aboveground private** parking only if you want $8,900/m² and 10-week processing — mixing in a public park or a basement-GFA grab knocks you into case-by-case premium.

---

### Source

- `PN 9_2025.pdf` (LAO PN No. 9/2025, 4 November 2025)
- Read with: **PNAP APP-2** (Nov 2025) paras 15–18 and Appendix C; superseded **LAO PN 3/2025**. Lease SC templates: Appendices I–II of this PN.
*Source Critical Summary:* `source_md/LAO Practice Note No. 9_2025 (November 2025)_CS.md`

<a id="pn-10-2025"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 10/2025 — Offering Private Land to Offset Land Premium for ECNTA Land Exchange or LSLD Tender in Northern Metropolis NDAs
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
30 December 2025 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Issue No. 10/2025

> Scope note: Lets ECNTA land-exchange applicants and LSLD tenderers in **Northern Metropolis NDAs** offset **premium balance** with the EGA value of **agricultural land** on a LandsD designated-projects list. It is **not** a planning or GFA PN. Land surrendered **in exchange for** the ECNTA site itself is **excluded**.

---

## Regulatory Overview

Applicants for lease modification including land exchange under the Enhanced Conventional New Town Approach (ECNTA — **LAO PN 13/2023**) or tenderers for grant of land by tender under Large-scale Land Disposal (LSLD) in NM NDAs may offer private land that is planned to be resumed, or has been resumed with EGA not yet released, to offset the balance of land premium payable ("Premium Offsetting").

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. What land can be offered (para 2)

| Test | Lock |
|---|---|
| Type | **Agricultural land** — old schedule and new grant lots **demised for agricultural use** |
| Location | Inside the **Northern Metropolis**; **excludes** any private land (agricultural or otherwise) to be **surrendered in exchange** for an ECNTA site |
| Owner as of the **relevant date** | ECNTA: date the PN inviting applications is promulgated. LSLD: date of tender invitation. Owner = applicant/tenderer **or** its **associated company** (Cap. 622). Land already resumed with EGA not yet released is **deemed** still owned by that former owner |
| Designated list | Must appear on LandsD's **list of designated projects** (land that **may be resumed within three years** of the relevant date, **or** already resumed with EGA outstanding). Published at landsd.gov.hk (land-acq-clearance / NDA page) |

Resumption ordinances cited: Cap. 124, Cap. 370, Cap. 370 as applied by s.26 of Cap. 358AL, Cap. 519.

**SD takeaway:** Only **NM agricultural** lots on the **designated list**, held by the applicant (or Cap. 622 associate) at invitation date, can offset premium — do not count the ECNTA surrender lots themselves.

---

### 2. Offer, title, resumption mechanics (paras 3–5)

Offers (ownership and particulars of each Offered Land) go in **with** the ECNTA application or LSLD tender. **No** later change of ownership or particulars. Prove title and area to LandsD; if owned by an associate, prove Cap. 622 association. Delay/failure may mean refusal of the Offered Land.

If as-yet-unresumed Offered Land is accepted: resumption notice gazetted/served/affixed; ownership reverts to Government **within one month** of the notice; Government clears households/businesses on prevailing NDA practice. Former owners must sign an **Agreement as to Compensation and Indemnity (ACI)** within **one month** of the resumption notice (or other period specified), agreeing the para 6 value for offsetting and warranting title free from encumbrances.

**SD takeaway:** Freeze Offered Land particulars **at application/tender** — post-offer transfers kill the offset.

---

### 3. Value and how it pays premium (paras 6–8)

| Item | Lock |
|---|---|
| Valuation basis | **Tier One or Tier Two** zone ex-gratia compensation rate for resumption of agricultural land, prevailing as of the date the resumption notice is **affixed** **or** the date of **reversion**, **whichever is the higher**. Rates depend on whether resumption is for development (e.g. NDAs) or non-development (e.g. wetland conservation) |
| Reference rates (from **1 October 2025**) | Tier One **$1,008 / sq ft**; Tier Two **$504 / sq ft** (subject to change) |
| Interest | Calculated value includes interest (if any) accrued before EGA release; for Premium Offsetting, payment is **deemed** on the date of **signing/execution of the ACI** |
| **ECNTA** | Offset must complete within a time stipulated in the **ECNTA invitation PN**. EGA in the executed ACI offsets premium **balance**; any shortfall is **cash** by LandsD's designated deadline |
| **LSLD** | Tenderers may opt **Staged Payment** instead of lump sum. **Only** the successful tenderer who opted Staged Payment may settle the remaining premium balance — **at most 75%** of total land premium (or as in Tender Documents) — by Premium Offsetting within **three years** from tender award (or as in Tender Documents). Parent-company written guarantee required |
| LSLD alienation | Until remaining premium is fully settled (offset or cash): **no alienation** of the tender-granted land, including underletting or pre-sale, **except building mortgage** for development |
| EGA cash | EGA accepted for offsetting is **not** paid in cash. Government may resume NM NDA land anyway; LandsD’s accept/reject of Offered Land is **final** |

**SD takeaway:** Size the offset against **Tier 1 $1,008/sf or Tier 2 $504/sf** (from 1 Oct 2025) of listed agri land; LSLD still needs **≥ 25% cash** up front unless the tender says otherwise, and **no pre-sale** until the remainder is paid.

---

### Source

- `PN 10_2025.pdf` (LAO PN No. 10/2025, 30 December 2025)
- Read with: **LAO PN 13/2023** (ECNTA); LandsD designated-projects list; Caps. **124 / 370 / 358AL / 519**.
*Source Critical Summary:* `source_md/LAO Practice Note No. 10_2025 (30 December 2025)_CS.md`

---

<a id="pn-1-2026"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 1/2026 — Certificate of Compliance applications
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
January 2026 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Supersedes LAO PN Nos. **1/1987** and **8/2000**; varies **PN 4/2008**

> Scope note: **Mandatory compliance checklist** for CC after completing a lease development. **Does not apply** to Cap. 121 NTEHs (use PN 1/2025). Phased lots with LandsD-approved Master Plans may get a **section** CC.

---

## Regulatory Overview

The optional checklist used since December 2000 is now **mandatory**, AP-confirmed, against a LandsD sample (App I, not exhaustive). Target: **CC within 10 weeks** or departmental comments within **8 weeks** of a valid application; unresolved conflicts may escalate under **DEVB General Circular No. 1/2026**.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. Documents that must accompany the CC application (para 4)

| Item | Who / what |
|---|---|
| (a) Completed **Checklist** + 4th-column proofs | AP confirms correctness |
| (b) AP letter (App II) | Development built to the **full set of latest building plans approved by LandsD** |
| (c) ALS letter + final survey report + Survey Record Plan (App III) | Certify **no encroachment** onto Government land (except BD+LandsD-approved projections/works over GL) |
| (d) | **Occupation Permit** and Certificate of Completion (where applicable) |
| (e) | LandsD **approval letter(s)** for that latest GBP set |
| (f) | Government rent paid to application date or last quarter |

Fn 6: because AP + ALS now certify as-built vs LandsD plans and no GL encroachment, **PN 4/2008 para 5 no longer applies** to CC applications on or after this PN.

**SD takeaway:** CC is blocked until LandsD-approved GBPs, OP, ALS no-encroachment survey, and AP as-built letter exist — freeze the LandsD plan set as the as-built datum from GBP stage.

---

### 2. Sample checklist — lease obligations LandsD will tick (App I)

Architect-facing rows (authority in brackets):

| Obligation | Typical authority |
|---|---|
| Premium; rent; no GL encroachment; building covenant | D of Lands / R&V |
| Green Area formation | HyD, TD, FSD, DSD, WSD, LCSD, CEDD |
| Brown Area | LandsD / BD / HAD |
| BO compliance | BD — submit **full latest BA-approved GBPs** |
| TPO / planning conditions | PlanD — **PlanD written confirmation** of approval-condition compliance |
| Tree preservation | LandsD — **PN 6/2023** |
| LMP / LP | PlanD or LandsD — **PN 1/2020 and 1/2020A** |
| Design and disposition | LandsD |
| SBD (setback, separation, greenery) | BD |
| Parking / L/UL / lay-bys; night-time CV parking; parking information; car-park layout deposit | TD / LandsD |
| Automatic meter reading (fresh water) | WSD |
| Babycare room (commercial accommodation) | DH |
| Footbridge / subway / pedestrian link | TD, HyD, CEDD, PlanD |
| **Government Accommodation** + assignment | GPA, ArchSD, plus TD/HyD for PTI; SWD for elderly centre; etc. |
| Public open space; public-facility layout; public vehicle park | Initiating B/D / TD |
| Green hatched black; natural terrain | GEO, CEDD |
| Drains/channels; drainage reserve; waterworks reserve; DIAs | DSD / WSD |
| TIA/traffic review; NIA; noise barrier; SIA; AQIA | TD / EPD / DSD |
| Restriction on alienation before compliance; DMC & MA | LandsD |

**SD takeaway:** Build a CC matrix at SD covering **GA, coloured areas, LMP/trees, SBD greenery, parking, POS/PVP, and planning conditions** — each needs a named department’s proof before the 10-week clock works.

---

### 3. Processing target and escalation (paras 6–7)

| Target | Clock |
|---|---|
| CC issued | **10 weeks** from valid application |
| Or comments relayed | **8 weeks** |
| Deadlock | LandsD coordinates; may escalate per **DEVB GC 1/2026** (large-scale private developments) |
| Effect | Paras 4–6 from **16 January 2026** |

**SD takeaway:** Programme CC (and thus DMC/sales) on a 10-week LandsD clock **after** a complete AP/ALS pack, with DEVB escalation only for remaining B/D deadlocks.

---

### Source

- `PN 1_2026.pdf`
- Supersedes PN **1/1987**, **8/2000**; varies **PN 4/2008**. Read with: **PN 1/2020 / 1/2020A**, **PN 6/2023**, **DEVB GC 1/2026**; Cap. 121 cases use **PN 1/2025**
*Source Critical Summary:* `source_md/LAO Practice Note No. 1-2026 (January 2026)_CS.md`

<a id="pn-2-2026"></a>

## LAO Practice Note No. 2/2026 — Pilot “Pay for What You Build” (non-residential)
**Architect critical summary for schematic design**  
May 2026 | Lands Administration Office — Lands Department  
Applications **1 June 2026 – 31 May 2029** (both inclusive) | Read with **LAO PN No. 2/2023**

> Scope note: Three-year pilot lease modification / land exchange so a lot owner pays premium only on an **Initial Phase** of **non-residential** GFA (≥ 60% of max), with a **ten-year** window after OP to buy the rest. It is **not** extra PR beyond OZP/BO, and **not** for residential GFA.

---

## Regulatory Overview

Applies territory-wide to non-residential developments on lots zoned Commercial, Industrial, OU (annotation), etc. On mixed zones (e.g. OU(Mixed Uses)), only the **independently developable non-residential portion** (no residential elements) may use the Pilot. Preferred use must be OZP-permitted or TPB-approved; LandsD decides the lease user wording.

---

## Critical main topics and subtopics

### 1. Initial Phase gates (paras 4–5)

| Gate | Lock |
|---|---|
| Intensity | Proposed Initial Phase GFA ≥ **60%** of “maximum development intensity” = total permissible max **GFA of the whole lot** under the **statutory town plan or Cap. 123, whichever lesser**. If TPB permission covers the development, max = **TPB-approved GFA**. Show intended Initial Phase GFA in the planning application where permission is needed |
| User | **Well-defined** preferred non-residential use with supporting information (scope, occupation mode, business model — App I note) |
| Lease terms on approval | **Building covenant** for Initial Phase; **restriction on alienation except as a whole** for the whole lot (or the non-res carve-out in a mixed zone); user clause = approved preferred use; **max GFA** = approved Initial Phase intensity |
| Mixed-zone carve-out (fn 4) | If ≥ 60% GFA can be carved as a **standalone lot** under a separate lease, LandsD will consider separately |
| Premium | Difference in market value **before vs after** modification, based on **full MV of the Initial Phase GFA** (i.e. at least 60%) and the approved preferred use |

**SD takeaway:** Do not draw an Initial Phase below **60% of the lesser of OZP and BO GFA**, and expect a **no-strata-sale** lock on the whole (non-res) lot until Remaining Balance is taken up or the ten-year period expires.

---

### 2. Remaining Balance GFA — ten years from first OP (paras 7–10)

| Item | Lock |
|---|---|
| Remaining Balance | **40% or less** of total permissible max GFA |
| Clock start | **Fulfilment of BC** evidenced by date of **first Occupation Permit** for the Initial Phase |
| To take up residual | Further lease modification **within ten years**, subject to **then** OZP/BO; premium at **then** full MV. Whole-lot alienation restriction **may** be removed if Remaining Balance is **fully** realised |
| If unused after 10 years | Government **may redeploy** leftover intensity and infrastructure capacity to **other lots in the district** on those owners’ applications (planning permission/rezoning and updated IIAs may be required) |
| After 10 years, same owner wants residual | Approach **PlanD first**; then further modification under then OZP/BO; departments may require **updated infrastructure IAs** |
| Alienation restriction after 10 years | May apply to remove it even if residual unused; LandsD discretion; **full MV premium** + fees |

**SD takeaway:** Phase 1 must be a complete, BC’d, OP’d building at ≥ 60%; leftover GFA is a **10-year option**, not a reserved right — after that neighbours may absorb it.

---

### 3. Transparency (para 11)

LandsD will publish approved Pilot cases (location, user, Initial Phase intensity) and whether a further modification was filed in the Ten-year Period, so other owners can judge unused capacity.

**SD takeaway:** Assume Initial Phase GFA/user will be public; do not rely on unused residual remaining exclusive to the lot.

---

### 4. Application pack (Apps I–II) — skip privacy boilerplate

Must identify: OZP/DPAP zone; preferred non-res uses with GFA delineation sketches; total permissible max GFA (lesser of OZP/DPAP and BO) and **percentage** proposed; TPB letter if any; 1:1000 plan; ownership; OZP/DPAP extracts. Facilitate: assignment/Deed Poll plans; AP (or competent professional) sketch proposal; MLP / EIA / TIA / DIA.

Applicant must **acknowledge** that unused Remaining Balance may be redeployed after ten years.

**SD takeaway:** The first letter must state the **% of max GFA** and attach use-by-use GFA plans — that percentage is the Pilot eligibility test.

---

### Source

- `PN 2_2026.pdf`
- Read with: **LAO PN No. 2/2023** (transaction procedure)
*Source Critical Summary:* `source_md/LAO Practice Note No. 2-2026 (May 2026)_CS.md`
